To further extract detailed information from fundus images and reduce the generation of artifacts during the enhancement process, we propose a multi-scale attention-based stage to learn more useful features for cataract image enhancement. Experimental results on Kaggle and ODIR-5K demonstrate that our TSMSA-Net outperforms current state-of-the-art cataract fundus images enhancement even without paired images and exhibits certain generalization ability. Experimental results on Kaggle and ODIR-5K datasets indicate that our TSMSA-Net outperforms the current state-of-the-art methods for cataract fundus image enhancement, even in the absence of paired images. Additionally, it demonstrates a certain level of generalization capability. The enhancement also can improve the performance of vessel segmentation and classification in cataract images.

</details>


### [65] [Scene-Q: Confidence-Aware Coarse-to-Fine Querying of 3D Scenes with Selective VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20235)
*Juno Kim,Yesol Park,Hye-Jung Yoon,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Scene-Q，一个置信度感知的由粗到精查询框架，通过温度缩放归一化编码器分数，并仅为低置信度案例选择性调用推理VLM进行重排，在保持大部分查询走快速路径的同时，显著提升开放词汇3D实例分割与自然语言3D实例检索的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内移动机器人需要将自然语言查询锚定到一致3D地图的开放词汇场景理解能力。然而现有系统大多依赖基于对比图像-文本编码器的余弦相似度检索，虽然高效，但在标签近似同义或多个相似实例出现时十分脆弱，导致歧义和误检索。因此需要一种能在高效检索与可靠消歧之间取得平衡的查询框架。

Method: Scene-Q采用置信度感知的由粗到精查询框架：首先利用温度缩放对编码器（如对比图像-文本编码器）的相似度分数进行归一化以量化置信度；对高置信度查询直接采用快速检索回答；对低置信度（歧义）查询，从候选集中选取top-K个实例，利用原始多视角图像和实例边界框，调用推理VLM（视觉语言模型）进行上下文感知的重新排序，从而在低成本下实现精准消歧。

Result: Scene-Q在ScanNet200上的开放词汇3D实例分割和真实世界重建上的自然语言3D实例检索任务均取得提升，尤其在与空间和关系相关的查询上增益最大，同时保持相当比例的查询仍走快速检索路径（fast path），兼顾效率与精度。

Conclusion: Scene-Q验证了置信度感知的混合查询策略的有效性：通过温度缩放精确识别歧义查询，并仅对低置信度实例调用VLM进行基于多视角上下文的重排，以可忽略的额外成本显著提升开放词汇3D场景理解的鲁棒性，为移动机器人导航与交互提供了更可靠的查询方案。

Abstract: Indoor mobile robots require open-vocabulary scene understanding that grounds natural-language queries in a consistent 3D map. Many existing systems ultimately rely on cosine-similarity retrieval with contrastive image--text encoders, which is efficient but brittle when labels are near-synonymous or multiple similar instances appear. We present Scene-Q, a confidence-aware coarse-to-fine querying framework that normalizes encoder scores with temperature scaling and selectively invokes a reasoning VLM only for low-confidence cases. High-confidence queries are answered by fast retrieval, while ambiguous ones are reranked over a small top-K candidate set using the original multi-view images and instance bounding boxes, enabling context-aware disambiguation at low cost. Scene-Q improves open-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation on ScanNet200 and natural-language 3D instance retrieval on real-world reconstructions, with the largest gains on spatial and relational queries while keeping a substantial fraction of queries on the fast path.

</details>


### [66] [SAGE-Yoga: Multi-Cue Learning for Yoga Pose Classification and Joint-Level Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20245)
*Hung Le Chi,Khanh Minh Huynh,Long Nghia Tran Pham,Tan Phuc Huynh,Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE-Yoga是一个从单张RGB图像进行瑜伽体式分类与关节级纠错的统一粗到细框架，通过互补视觉特征集成、基于间隔的门控机制以及类特定关节角度分布对比，实现细粒度分类和可解释的纠正反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动瑜伽分析方法存在三点不足：单一视觉预测难以区分视觉相似的体式、将体式分类与纠正视为相互独立的任务、缺乏可解释的执行反馈。作者希望模拟瑜伽教练利用多种互补线索评估姿势的方式，建立一个统一框架同时解决分类与纠错问题。

Method: SAGE-Yoga采用粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的两阶段框架：第一阶段采用基于bagging的互补视觉骨干集成，生成排序后的候选体式类别集合；同时利用基于间隔的门控机制，对置信度高的视觉预测直接保留，仅对模糊案例触发几何验证。确定最终体式类别后，检索medoid参考体式，将观测关节角度与类特定分布进行比较以识别错位关节，最后将偏差转化为可操作的纠正反馈。

Result: 在Yoga-82数据集上，纯视觉集成达到89.0%的Top-1准确率；加入几何验证的完整框架将Top-1准确率提升至90.7%，Macro-F1达到90.1%。

Conclusion: 将互补视觉证据与选择性几何验证相结合，既能提升细粒度体式分类性能，又能实现可解释的关节级纠错，验证了该统一框架的有效性。

Abstract: Automated yoga analysis requires both accurate pose classification and interpretable feedback on pose execution. However, existing methods often rely on a single visual prediction, struggle to distinguish visually similar poses, and treat pose classification and correction as separate tasks. To address these limitations, we propose SAGE-Yoga, a unified coarse-to-fine framework for yoga pose classification and joint-level correction from a single RGB image. Inspired by how yoga instructors assess posture using multiple complementary cues, SAGE-Yoga first employs a bagging-based ensemble of complementary visual backbones to generate a ranked set of candidate pose classes. Additionally, a margin-based gating mechanism preserves confident visual predictions while invoking geometric verification only for ambiguous cases. Moreover, once the final pose class is determined, SAGE-Yoga retrieves a medoid reference pose and compares the observed joint angles with class-specific distributions to identify misaligned joints. Finally, these deviations are translated into actionable corrective feedback. Empirically, experiments on the Yoga-82 dataset show that the visual ensemble achieves 89.0% Top-1 accuracy, while the complete framework improves performance to 90.7% Top-1 accuracy and 90.1% Macro-F1. These results demonstrate that combining complementary visual evidence with selective geometric verification improves fine-grained pose classification while enabling interpretable, joint-level correction.

</details>


### [67] [Distance to Class Prototypes: Active Learning for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20248)
*Licheng Zhang,Zheng Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于深度目标检测器主动学习的新评分准则：通过在训练目标中加入监督对比项塑造每目标嵌入空间，使距离编码类别成员关系，并以预测类别区域的距离加权置信度来对未标注图像打分。该方法无需集成、辅助预测器或重复推理，仅增加2.89M参数（8.3%），在PASCAL VOC和MS-COCO上每轮选择均优于同一检测器的后验基线，最高提升1.08% mAP50，并与需要3-50次前向推理的集成及MC dropout准则保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全新的场景部署深度目标检测器时，主要瓶颈并非网络架构，而是标注数据的成本。主动学习通过挑选要标注的图像来降低成本，但其效果取决于对未标注图像的评分信号质量。现有的类后验信号廉价但校准差，而多模型或多随机前向的模型分歧信号效果好但推理成本是未标注图像数量的倍数。作者希望找到一种比后验更丰富、却只需单次单网络前向即可获得的评分信号。

Method: 在训练目标中增加监督对比项，塑造每个目标的嵌入空间，使嵌入距离编码类别成员关系。对未标注检测时，依据其嵌入与预测类别区域的距离，并以预测置信度为权重进行评分。该准则不需要集成、辅助预测器或重复推理，其全部附加代价仅为2.89M参数（较裸检测器增加8.3%）。实验采用单阶段检测器，使各比较准则在同一检测器下报告结果，从而将选择决策与检测器强度隔离。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC和MS-COCO上，该准则在每一轮选择中均优于同一检测器的类后验基线，最高提升1.08% mAP50（运行间偏差为0.02%-0.18%），并与每张未标注图像需3-50次前向推理的集成和蒙特卡洛dropout准则保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该准则证明：借助监督对比学习塑造的嵌入空间，可以用单次前向获得比类后验更丰富的评分信号，在不牺牲准确率的前提下大幅降低主动选择的计算成本，从而更高效地降低目标检测器在新场景中的标注开销。

Abstract: Deploying a deep object detector in a new setting is limited less by architecture than by the cost of annotating data from that setting. Active learning lowers the cost by choosing which images to label, and the choice is only as good as the signal used to score an unlabeled image. That signal is usually the class posterior, which is cheap but poorly calibrated, or the disagreement across several models or several stochastic passes, which is better but multiplies inference over a pool far larger than the labeled set. We propose a signal richer than the posterior yet still read from one forward pass of one network. A supervised contrastive term added to the training objective shapes a per-object embedding space in which distance encodes class membership, and an unlabeled detection is scored by how far it lies from the region occupied by its predicted category, weighted by its confidence. The criterion needs no ensemble, no auxiliary predictor and no repeated inference, and its entire cost is 2.89M parameters, an increase of 8.3% over a bare detector. On PASCAL VOC and MS-COCO it beats the posterior of the same detector in every round in which a selection is made, by up to 1.08% mAP50 against run to run deviations of 0.02% to 0.18%, and it stays competitive with ensemble and Monte Carlo dropout criteria costing three to fifty forward passes per unlabeled image. Experiments use the single-stage detector under which the compared criteria report their results, so that the selection decision is isolated from the strength of the detector.

</details>


### [68] [Generative Verification: Rethinking the Uncertainty Signal for Active Learning of Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20262)
*Licheng Zhang,Zheng Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的主动目标检测采集信号"生成式验证"：用一个独立的生成模型（条件扩散模型）从检测框内的像素重新推导标签，用它与检测器的分歧作为采集信号。该设计本身天然合并了分类与定位失败模式，并能优先采样"自信但错误"的高代价样本，在PASCAL VOC和MS-COCO上优于现有各类基于同一检测器自身不确定性的标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几乎所有现有主动目标检测的采集函数都共享同一安排：被改进的模型同时也是被询问的模型。这导致检测器的置信度偏差会自我强化——对"自信但错误"（confidently wrong）的检测，自采样的信号会认为其无趣，而这类样本恰恰是最不值得遗漏的（成本最高）。作者希望打破这一同源限制，引入一个独立于被测模型的信号源。

Method: 核心是"生成式验证"框架：保留待改进的检测器，同时构建一个独立的生成式验证器——一个以标签表示（而非图像）为扩散目标的条件扩散模型。验证器从检测框内的像素重新推导标签，其随机逆过程可以重复采样得到标签分布，分布集中度衡量证据对标签的确定程度（而分类器只给出单点估计），检测器输出与验证器推导之间的分歧即为采集信号。

Result: 该方法在PASCAL VOC和MS-COCO上全面优于输出不确定性、特征几何、扰动和集成等主动检测标准，在MS-COCO上每轮约提升1个mAP50点，其优势在早期轮次（自信的检测器错误最频繁时）最为显著。由于位移框、背景框及标签错误的正确框都会导致裁剪无法通过验证，分类与定位两类失败模式天然合并为一个标量，无需手调权重。

Conclusion: 通过引入与被测检测器相互独立的生成式验证器，主动目标检测不再依赖检测器自身的自信度自我评估，从而能够将"自信但错误"的高代价样本纳入采集，获得更稳健的主动学习收益。该信号的优势源于框架的构造本身而非参数调优，且扩散模型提供的分布式证据比分类器的点估计更能刻画标签确定性。

Abstract: Nearly every acquisition function for active object detection shares one arrangement, in that the model being improved is also the model being interrogated. We depart from it. In generative verification an independent generative model re-derives the label of a detection from the pixels inside its predicted box, and the disagreement between the two becomes the acquisition signal. Two properties follow from the arrangement itself rather than from any tuning. A displaced box, a box on background and a correct box carrying the wrong label all yield a crop that fails verification, so the failure modes arrive already combined in one scalar and the hand-weighted classification and localization terms of existing criteria are no longer needed. And because the verifier never observes the detector confidence, confidently wrong detections score highest, although a self-derived signal reads them as uninteresting and they are the costliest to leave unlabeled. We build the verifier as a conditional diffusion model whose diffusion target is a label representation rather than an image. Its reverse process is stochastic, so repeated generations return a distribution whose concentration reports how firmly the evidence determines the label, where a classifier returns a single point estimate. On PASCAL VOC and MS-COCO the signal outperforms output-uncertainty, feature-geometry, perturbation and ensemble criteria, gaining about one mAP50 point per round on MS-COCO, with its largest margins in the early rounds where confident detector errors are most common.

</details>


### [69] [AI or Real: Detecting Partially Altered Videos Under Resource-Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20263)
*Tamoghna Chakraborty,Md Nurul Absur,Sourya Saha,Saptarshi Debroy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级全帧检测器，用于检测边缘硬件上部分篡改的AI生成视频，无需人脸检测预处理。该系统通过蒸馏将DINOv2-Base教师模型的知识迁移到冻结的MobileNetV3-Small学生模型，结合温度退火软标签迁移、注意力多样性正则化、帧级监督和残差特征适配器。针对部分篡改场景的两个失败模式（真实场景切换的误报、主导纯真实类别的阈值误校准）分别通过视频内时间硬负样本和校准感知采样解决。在包含55,393样本的拼接测试集上（伪造帧比例6.2%~31.2%），学生模型缩小了与教师模型58%的差距（AUC 0.766），在RTX A4000上每16帧片段推理耗时3.65毫秒，检查点仅150.4 MB，适配边缘设备内存和延迟预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频模型的普及使得实际检测威胁从完全伪造片段转向部分篡改内容。现有现代检测器虽利用4亿+参数的基础骨干达到高精度，但资源占用过大，无法在边缘设备部署。因此需要一种轻量级且无需人脸检测预处理的检测方案，以实现在边缘硬件上对部分篡改AI视频进行高效检测。

Method: 系统采用知识蒸馏流程，将DINOv2-Base教师蒸馏到冻结的MobileNetV3-Small学生模型。蒸馏管线组合了温度退火软标签迁移、注意力多样性正则化、帧级监督以及残差特征适配器（用于将ImageNet特征条件化为工件检测）。此外，针对部分篡改场景的两个特定失败模式设计对策：使用视频内时间硬负样本解决真实场景切换导致的误报；采用校准感知采样解决主导纯真实类别上的阈值级误校准。

Result: 在包含55,393个样本、伪造帧比例从6.2%到31.2%的拼接测试集上，学生模型缩小了与DINOv2-Base教师模型58%的性能差距（AUC为0.766）。推理速度在RTX A4000上达到每16帧片段3.65毫秒，检查点大小为150.4 MB，满足边缘设备的显存和延迟预算。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级全帧检测器在保持较高检测性能（AUC 0.766，接近教师58%的性能差距）的同时，实现了适合边缘部署的资源占用（150.4 MB检查点，3.65 ms/16帧推理），有效解决了部分篡改视频检测在边缘硬件上的部署难题，并通过时间硬负样本和校准感知采样针对性缓解了场景切换误报与阈值误校准问题。

Abstract: The proliferation of generative video models has shifted the practical detection threat from fully fabricated clips to partially manipulated footages. Although modern detectors achieve strong accuracy using foundation backbones of 400M+ parameters, their resource footprint precludes edge deployment. In this paper, we present a lightweight full-frame detector for partially manipulated AI-generated video, designed for deployment on edge hardware without face-detection preprocessing. The system distills a DINOv2-Base teacher into a frozen MobileNetV3-Small student through a pipeline that combines temperature-annealed soft-label transfer, attention-diversity regularization, frame-level supervision, and a residual feature adapter that conditions ImageNet features for artifact detection. We additionally target two failure modes specific to the partial-manipulation regime: false positives on legitimate scene cuts, addressed through within-video temporal hard negatives; and threshold-level miscalibration on the dominant pure-real class, addressed through calibration-aware sampling. Evaluation on a 55,393-sample spliced test set across fake-frame ratios from 6.2% to 31.2% demonstrates the student model closing 58% of the gap to the DINOv2-Base teacher (AUC 0.766) while running at 3.65 ms per 16-frame clip on RTX A4000 with a 150.4 MB checkpoint compatible with edge-device memory and latency budgets.

</details>


### [70] [CleanVideo: Adaptive Concept Erasure for Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20267)
*Junchi Liao,Hongji Li,Wenrui Zhou,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CleanVideo，一种针对视频扩散模型的低维子空间选择性概念擦除框架，通过三模态门控机制（时空视觉特征、时间步信号、文本语义）决定擦除的"位置、时机与是否"进行干预，在保持视觉保真度和时间一致性的同时有效擦除目标概念，优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念擦除旨在从预训练生成模型中选择性消除不期望的视觉语义而不损害其通用能力。然而将概念擦除从图像扩展到视频具有挑战性：目标概念在视频中逐渐涌现且随帧和去噪步骤变化，固定干预可能导致目标遗漏或引入模糊、抖动和内容失真。

Method: CleanVideo采用低维子空间干预，由三模态门控机制控制。该机制联合处理时空视觉特征、时间步信号和文本语义，动态确定"哪里、何时、是否"进行干预。当自然替代概念可被清晰定义时，将擦除内容引导至替代概念，同时保留非目标内容。

Result: 在三个视频扩散模型上的实验表明，CleanVideo能有效擦除目标概念，同时保持视觉保真度和时间一致性；在帧级与视频级评估中均优于现有基线，且在概念恢复攻击下（受保护管线保持完整时）表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过自适应门控与低维子空间干预，CleanVideo解决了视频概念擦除中概念随时间演变带来的动态干预难题，兼顾擦除效果与视频质量，为视频生成模型的内容安全提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Concept erasure aims to selectively eliminate undesired visual semantics from pre-trained generative models without compromising their general utility. Extending concept erasure from images to video is nontrivial. Target concepts emerge gradually and vary across frames and denoising steps. As a result, fixed interventions may miss the target or introduce blurring, jitter, and content distortion. We propose CleanVideo, a selective erasure framework that performs low-dimensional subspace intervention controlled by a tri-modal gating mechanism. By jointly processing spatiotemporal visual features, timestep signals, and textual semantics, CleanVideo determines where, when, and whether to intervene, steering erased content toward natural surrogate concepts when such surrogates can be clearly defined while preserving non-target content. Experiments on three video diffusion models show that CleanVideo effectively erases target concepts while maintaining visual fidelity and temporal coherence, outperforming existing baselines under frame-level and video-level evaluations and under concept-recovery attacks when the protected pipeline remains intact.

</details>


### [71] [Queries Knew More Than We Thought: Uncovering Latent Knowledge in Segmentation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20283)
*Ignacio M. De la Jara,Cristian Rodriguez-Opazo,Damith Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结的DETR系列分割模型中的"输出选择瓶颈"问题：模型已经计算出有用的掩码，但部署时的选择规则没有将其暴露出来。作者提出HYDRA——一个仅基于缓存冻结输出训练的小型选择器，在推理时对缓存候选进行打分并与保留基线的显式选项比较，仅在置信度足够时替换输出。实验在ADE20k和COCO上使Mask2Former、MaskDINO和OneFormer提升最高+7.41数据集mIoU；在八个域上使SAM 3平均提升+9.4类宏prompt-IoU。对照实验表明轻量级权重适配无法消除瓶颈，而对适配候选进行路由仍能恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分割器常在昂贵计算完成后仍然失败——有用的掩码其实已存在于模型基于查询条件的候选中，但部署的选择规则没有将其暴露。作者研究冻结DETR族模型中的这一输出选择瓶颈，并质疑是否可以在不增加查询、不生成新掩码、不重跑骨干网络、不更新权重的情况下恢复这部分隐藏的性能余量。

Method: HYDRA是一个仅在缓存冻结输出上训练的小型选择器。推理时，它对缓存候选打分，并与一个显式的"保留基线"选项（keep-baseline）比较，仅在留出数据上校准的边际表明所选候选明显更好时才采取行动。模型在训练集缓存上训练，并在留出数据上校准，从而在提升精度的同时保留有用预测。作者还通过配对LoRA对照实验和受控TinyDETR研究来验证瓶颈的存在及其与二分匹配下查询特化的联系。

Result: HYDRA在ADE20k和COCO上使Mask2Former、MaskDINO和OneFormer提升最高+7.41数据集mIoU；使SAM 3在八个域上平均提升+9.4类宏prompt-IoU。配对LoRA对照显示轻量级权重适配并不能消除瓶颈——暴露的预测往往持平或更差，而对适配候选进行路由仍能恢复精度。

Conclusion: 冻结分割器不仅应根据其暴露的掩码来评估，还应依据其抑制的有用候选来评估。输出选择瓶颈可以通过仅基于缓存输出的轻量级校准选择器得到缓解，且权重适配无法替代对候选的路由选择。该效应与二分匹配下的查询特化机制相关联，并在受控实验中得到了验证。

Abstract: Modern segmenters often fail after the expensive computation has already been done: a useful mask is present among the model's query-conditioned candidates, but the deployed selection rule does not expose it. We study this output-selection bottleneck in frozen DETR-family models. A ground-truth-only oracle first shows substantial hidden headroom in already-computed mask proposals. This raises a simple question: How can we better use the masks a segmenter has already computed but does not expose? We then ask whether that headroom can be recovered without adding queries, generating new masks, rerunning the backbone, or updating weights. HYDRA is a small selector trained only on cached frozen outputs. At inference time, it scores the cached candidates against an explicit keep-baseline option and acts only when a held-out calibrated margin indicates the selected candidate is sufficiently better. Trained on training-split caches and calibrated on held-out data, HYDRA improves Mask2Former, MaskDINO, and OneFormer by up to +7.41 dataset mIoU points on ADE20k and COCO, and improves SAM 3 by +9.4 class-macro prompt-IoU points on average across eight domains while preserving useful predictions through calibration. Paired LoRA controls show that lightweight weight adaptation does not remove the bottleneck: exposed predictions are often flat or worse, while routing over the adapted candidates still recovers accuracy. Finally, we connect the effect to query specialization under bipartite matching and verify it in a controlled TinyDETR study. These results show that frozen segmenters should be evaluated not only by the masks they expose, but also by the useful candidates they suppress.

</details>


### [72] [TinyCNN: A 193K-Parameter Network for On-Device Plant Disease Detection, with a Cross-Dataset Robustness Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20290)
*Ngoc-Bao Ho-Lam,Thai-Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TinyCNN，一种面向设备端植物病害分类的轻量级卷积神经网络，仅含193,190个可训练参数与110.05M MACs，在38类PlantVillage基准上达到98.88%测试准确率与98.03%宏F1，比ResNet18小约58倍、比MobileNetV2教师小11.8倍。经知识蒸馏（alpha=0.3, T=4）获得98.81%准确率；跨数据集评估（PlantVillage→PlantDoc）揭示真实场景下显著鲁棒性差距，Grad-CAM分析表明注意力偏离叶片、受背景驱动，符合捷径学习特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期农作物病害检测对实现联合国可持续发展目标2（零饥饿）下的可持续农业与粮食安全至关重要，尤其在资源受限地区——专家诊断稀缺而低成本移动设备普及。作者旨在通过低能耗、可部署的模型压缩策略（轻量网络与知识蒸馏），降低推理的能量、内存与成本开销，符合Green AI原则，同时解决设备端病害分类的实际需求。

Method: 设计了基于深度可分离卷积模块的轻量CNN（TinyCNN），参数规模仅193,190、MACs为110.05M（224×224输入），并作为学生网络采用vanilla知识蒸馏技术从MobileNetV2教师迁移知识；对蒸馏超参数alpha∈{0.3,0.5,0.7}与T∈{2,4}做消融实验，并利用Grad-CAM进行跨数据集可解释性分析以诊断鲁棒性差距的成因。

Result: TinyCNN在38类PlantVillage上达到98.88%测试准确率、98.03%宏F1，体积约为ResNet18的1/58、MobileNetV2教师的1/11.8。最优蒸馏配置（alpha=0.3, T=4）得到98.81%准确率。跨数据集从PlantVillage迁移至PlantDoc时出现显著鲁棒性下降，Grad-CAM显示模型注意力集中在叶片之外、受背景驱动，归因于捷径学习。

Conclusion: TinyCNN是一个能效高、可部署的可持续农业智能构建块，显著压缩了模型体积以契合Green AI与边缘部署需求；但真实世界条件下的领域鲁棒性仍是通过耐久实际影响的关健障碍，需在后续工作中解决背景驱动注意力与捷径学习问题。

Abstract: Detecting crop disease early is central to sustainable agriculture and food security under United Nations Sustainable Development Goal 2 (Zero Hunger), and is especially urgent in resource-constrained regions where expert diagnosis is scarce but low-cost mobile devices are widespread. This paper presents TinyCNN, a lightweight convolutional neural network for on-device plant disease classification. TinyCNN uses depthwise separable convolution blocks and contains only 193,190 trainable parameters with 110.05M MACs for a 224x224 input image. On the 38-class PlantVillage benchmark, TinyCNN achieves 98.88% test accuracy and 98.03% macro-F1 while being approximately 58x smaller than ResNet18 and 11.8x smaller than a MobileNetV2 teacher, directly reducing the energy, memory, and cost footprint of inference in line with Green AI principles. The paper further analyzes vanilla knowledge distillation as a sustainable model-compression strategy; an ablation over alpha in {0.3, 0.5, 0.7} and T in {2, 4} selects alpha=0.3, T=4, producing a distilled TinyCNN with 98.81% test accuracy. Finally, cross-dataset evaluation from PlantVillage to PlantDoc reveals a substantial robustness gap under real-world conditions, which a Grad-CAM analysis attributes to off-leaf, background-driven attention consistent with shortcut learning. TinyCNN is thus an energy-efficient, deployable building block for sustainable agricultural intelligence, while field robustness remains the key barrier to durable real-world impact.

</details>


### [73] [Not All Layers Are Equal: Dynamic Layer Routing for Reliable CLIP OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20299)
*Ignacio M. De la Jara,Cristian Rodriguez-Opazo,Damith Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Voyager 方法，将 OOD 检测中层信息聚合问题重构为"哪些层值得信任"的可学习层选择问题。通过弱 OOD 上下文生成方法提供监督，并学习一个轻量级路由器在 CLIP 的层深度上选择稀疏的、锚定于最终层的专家。在三个基准上提升 OOD 检测性能，训练仅需约 2 分钟、<1GB 内存，比现有提示学习方法高效约 20 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已证明跨模型层的信息聚合能提升 OOD 检测，但此前研究聚焦于"如何融合各层"，缺乏对"哪些层值得信任"这一问题的探讨。作者希望将层选择视为可学习问题，从而避免设计复杂的融合机制，并探究监督来源与路由组件各自的贡献。

Method: Voyager 采用弱 OOD 上下文生成方法进行监督（被证明比现有 SOTA 方法机制更有效），并学习一个轻量级路由器，在 CLIP 的层深度上选择稀疏的、锚定于最终层的专家组合，用于 OOD 检测。

Result: 在三个多样化基准上 Voyager 提升了 OOD 检测性能。在 ImageNet-1K 上达到平均 FPR@95 为 18.86，比最强、可比的提示学习方法高出 8.8 个点。性能提升在多种监督来源下均保持，说明关键优势来自可学习路由器组件而非监督来源。训练约 2 分钟、<1GB 内存，比现有提示学习方法高效约 20 倍。

Conclusion: 将 OOD 检测中的层信息聚合问题转化为可学习的层选择问题（即以路由器挑选可信层）是可行且高效的。Voyager 证明弱监督仅起辅助作用，核心收益来自可学习的稀疏层选择路由机制，同时具备极强的实用性与训练效率。

Abstract: Information aggregation across model layers are revealed to improve OOD detection. In contrast to crafting a method for layer-wise information aggregation in recent work, we investigate if layer selection is a learnable problem. In other words, we transpose the question from how to fuse layers to one asking which layers to trust for an input. Using a generalizable, weak, out of distribution context crafting approach for supervision, shown to be more effective than state of the art methods' mechanisms, we formulate learning a lightweight router to select a sparse, final-layer-anchored expert over CLIP's layer depth for OOD detection.
  Across three diverse benchmarks we demonstrate our learnable routing method dubbed Voyager improves OOD detection. On ImageNet-1K, Voyager achieves an average FPR@95 of 18.86, outperforming the strongest, comparable, prompt-learning method by 8.8 points. These gains persist across multiple supervision sources, including those used by existing state-of-the-art prompt-learning methods, demonstrating that, whilst our weak OOD supervision context is highly effective, the key advantage is realized from the learnable router component rather than the supervision source. Significantly, Voyager is highly practical; router learning takes approximately two minutes using less than 1 GB of memory, making it approximately 20x more efficient than current prompt-learning approaches.
  Anonymized Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Voyager/

</details>


### [74] [AgriScope: Pixel-Grounded Multimodal Understanding for Agricultural Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20325)
*Abderrahmene Boudiaf,Mohamad Alanssari,Irfan Hussain,Sajid Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgriScope，一个统一像素级接地（pixel-grounded）的多模态农业图像理解框架，同时支持图像级、区域级和像素级理解，涵盖接地字幕生成、指代表达分割和多轮多模态交互。配套发布 AgriGround 大规模像素接地指令微调数据集（超50万图像、1100万指令样本），由多阶段自动标注流水线构建，实验证明其在多个农业视觉-语言任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业图像理解需要在复杂真实场景下对植物病害、虫害、作物结构及植物物种进行细粒度识别。尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）近年取得进展，现有模型仅支持文本输出，缺乏像素级视觉接地能力，无法实现精细的空间定位与分割，难以满足农业领域的稠密理解需求。

Method: AgriScope 采用统一框架联合支持图像级、区域级和像素级理解，通过生物语义编码（biological-semantic encoding）、稠密空间表示（dense spatial representations）和像素解码（pixel decoding）将生物专业语义表示与稠密空间接地相结合。同时提出 AgriGround 数据集，通过多阶段自动标注流水线整合多模态字幕生成、短语级接地、分割掩码生成和面向任务的指令合成，产生稠密接地监督信号。

Result: 在多个农业视觉-语言任务上的广泛实验表明，AgriScope 在像素级接地多模态理解方面表现有效，建立了农业视觉-语言学习与视觉接地任务的强基准。数据集和代码将公开于 GitHub。

Conclusion: AgriScope 填补了 MLLMs 在农业领域缺乏像素级视觉接地能力的空白，通过统一的像素接地框架和大规模高质量数据集（AgriGround），显著提升了农业图像在病害分析、作物与杂草识别、害虫识别及细粒度植物理解等任务上的接地理解水平，为农业视觉-语言学习提供了可复现的基准与开放资源。

Abstract: Agricultural image understanding requires fine-grained recognition of plant diseases, pests, crop structures, and botanical species under complex real-world conditions. Despite recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), existing models remain limited to text-only outputs and lack pixel-level visual grounding capabilities. In this work, we introduce AgriScope, a unified pixel-grounded multimodal framework for agricultural image understanding. AgriScope jointly supports image-level, region-level, and pixel-level understanding within a unified framework, enabling tasks such as grounded caption generation, referring expression segmentation, and multi-turn multimodal interaction for agricultural imagery. AgriScope integrates biologically specialized semantic representations with dense spatial grounding through biological-semantic encoding, dense spatial representations, and pixel decoding. To support large-scale grounded learning, we introduce AgriGround, a large-scale pixel-grounded agricultural multimodal instruction-tuning dataset containing over 500K images and 11M instruction-following samples spanning plant disease analysis, crop and weed identification, insect pest recognition, and fine-grained botanical understanding. AgriGround is constructed through a multi-stage automatic annotation pipeline that integrates multimodal caption generation, phrase-level grounding, segmentation mask generation, and task-oriented instruction synthesis to produce densely grounded supervision. Extensive experiments across multiple agricultural vision-language tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of AgriScope in pixel-grounded multimodal understanding, establishing a strong benchmark for agricultural vision-language learning and visual grounding. The dataset and code will be made publicly available at (https://github.com/boudiafA/AgriScope)

</details>


### [75] [FreqDINO++: A Frequency-Guided Multi-Task Routing Vision Foundation Model for Universal Ultrasound Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20340)
*Qing Xu,Yixuan Zhang,Yue Li,Xiangjian He,Qian Zhang,Mainul Haque,Rong Qu,Wenting Duan,Jieyun Bai,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FreqDINO++，一个基于频率引导的多任务路由视觉基础模型，用于通用超声分析。该模型通过 MR-Adapter（多任务路由适配器）实现参数高效的任务通用与任务特定知识整合，通过 F²-Enhancer（频率感知特征增强器）捕捉超声图像的多尺度频率特征，并通过 TC-Decoder（任务对齐协同解码器）促进密集预测与全局预测任务间的协作。在大型多任务和外部单任务超声基准上的大量实验表明，FreqDINO++ 在 27 个多样化临床任务场景中一致优于强基线和近期基础模型，并展现出对未见数据的良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声图像分析在癌症筛查和产前诊断中至关重要，但综合评估需要同时处理病灶分割和良恶性分类等异质任务。尽管近期视觉基础模型展现出强大的通用表征能力，但由于超声图像与自然图像之间存在显著的领域差距，其潜力难以发挥。现有方法通常针对孤立任务微调重型视觉编码器，不仅带来巨大的计算开销，还忽略了异质任务间的潜在共性，因此亟需一个高效的统一多任务框架。

Method: FreqDINO++ 采用三个核心组件：(1) 多任务路由适配器（MR-Adapter），通过参数高效方式整合任务通用知识与任务特定知识；(2) 频率感知特征增强器（F²-Enhancer），用于捕捉超声图像丰富的多尺度频率特征；(3) 任务对齐协同解码器（TC-Decoder），通过全局-局部令牌交互促进密集预测（如分割）与全局预测（如分类）任务间的协作。

Result: 在大型多任务超声基准和外部单任务超声基准上的大量实验表明，FreqDINO++ 在 27 个多样化临床任务场景中一致优于强基线和近期基础模型，同时展现出对未见数据的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: FreqDINO++ 通过频率引导的多任务路由机制，成功地弥合了视觉基础模型与超声领域之间的领域差距，在参数高效的前提下实现了分割与分类等多任务的联合优化，证明了通用超声统一分析框架的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Ultrasound image analysis plays a crucial role in cancer screening and prenatal diagnosis, yet comprehensive assessment requires jointly addressing tasks such as lesion segmentation and benign-malignant classification. While recent vision foundation models have shown remarkable universal representations, unlocking their potential for ultrasound is bottlenecked by the considerable domain gap from natural images. Existing methods typically fine-tune heavy vision encoders for isolated tasks, incurring substantial computational overhead while overlooking the underlying commonalities across heterogeneous tasks. In this work, we propose FreqDINO++, a frequency-guided multi-task routing vision foundation model for universal ultrasound analysis. We first introduce a Multi-task Routing Adapter (MR-Adapter) to support parameter-efficient integration of task-common and task-specific knowledge, a Frequency-aware Feature Enhancer (F$^2$-Enhancer) is then designed to capture the rich multi-scale frequency characteristics of ultrasound images, and a Task-aligned Collaborative Decoder (TC-Decoder) is devised to promote collaboration between dense and global prediction tasks through global-local token interaction. Extensive experiments on large-scale multi-task and external single-task ultrasound benchmarks demonstrate that FreqDINO++ consistently outperforms strong baselines and recent foundation models across 27 diverse clinical task scenarios, while also showing promising generalization to unseen data. The code is at https://github.com/MingLang-FD/FreqDINO-Plus.

</details>


### [76] [Fast Cross-Strength Multi-Contrast Brain MRI Translation using Latent Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20341)
*Siddharth Srivastava,Till Bretschneider*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于条件隐空间桥匹配（conditional latent bridge matching）的统一条件模型，用于可控的MRI场强间合成（field-to-field synthesis）。该单一模型无需任务专用架构或训练，即可在MRIxFields2026挑战赛的三个任务验证阶段中取得极具竞争力的结果，支持单步推理快速生成，并提供了丰富的消融实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同场强下采集的MRI在噪声、分辨率、均匀性和对比度上存在显著差异，这限制了跨采集设置的图像可比性，并使下游分析复杂化。现有方法缺乏统一的、可控的场强间合成方案，因此需要一个既能生成又好、又快的通用模型。

Method: 基于条件隐空间桥匹配（conditional latent bridge matching）框架构建单一统一的条件生成模型，实现可控的field-to-field合成。模型无需针对任务定制的架构或专门训练，即可覆盖挑战赛的所有三个任务；采用单步推理实现快速生成。

Result: 单一模型在MRIxFields2026挑战赛全部三个任务的验证阶段均取得高度竞争力结果。生成速度快：30个轴向切片的全部模态与场强组合在90秒内完成，全容积跨模态-场强翻译在70秒内完成（单块NVIDIA A5000 GPU）。此外提供了针对各组件的大量消融实验。

Conclusion: 条件隐空间桥匹配可作为MRI场强间合成的通用、统一且高效的解决方案，兼具出色的生成质量与极快的推理速度，并具备良好的可解释性与可扩展性（代码已开源）。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) acquired at different field strengths exhibits pronounced variation in noise, resolution, homogeneity, and contrast, which limits comparability across acquisition settings and complicates downstream analysis. We address this with a unified conditional model for controllable field-to-field synthesis, built on the framework of conditional latent bridge matching. Our single model achieves highly competitive results across the validation phase for all three tasks of the MRIxFields2026 challenge without task-specific architectures or training. We achieve fast generation with only a single inference step, producing all modality and field-strength combinations for $30$ axial slices in under $90$ seconds, as well as cross-modality-strength translation for a full volume in under $70$ seconds, on a single NVIDIA A5000 GPU. We further provide extensive ablations regarding different components of our solution. Code: https://gitlab.com/siddharthsrivastava/mrixfields-2026

</details>


### [77] [EliGSiR: Continual RGB-D Mapping with Gaussian Splatting under Bounded Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20348)
*Björn Ellensohn,Elmar Rueckert,Christian Rauch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EliGSiR 是一种面向持续 RGB-D 建图的增量式 3D 高斯泼溅方法，通过 Map-Guided View Scheduling（地图引导视图调度）、Load-Adaptive Fidelity（负载自适应保真度）和 Targeted Geometry Growth（目标几何增长）三种机制，在有限的优化预算下动态调配视图选择、监督细节和几何增长，从而在持续建图过程中兼顾新观测的融入与已重建区域的保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 3D 高斯泼溅假设观测集闭合且需长时间优化，而持续 RGB-D 建图面临新观测在线到达、同时须保留既有重建区域的挑战。现有方法难以在有限且变化的计算预算下高效管理不断增长的观测数据，因此需要一种能够根据建图状态自适应分配优化资源的持续式高斯建图器。

Method: 提出 EliGSiR，包含三个核心机制：(1) Map-Guided View Scheduling，依据当前地图状态过滤冗余的新视图并重新评估保留视图；(2) Load-Adaptive Fidelity，根据建图负载动态调整监督分辨率而非使用固定分辨率策略；(3) Targeted Geometry Growth，将深度监督与高斯生成解耦，仅当重复的 RGB-D 观测表明存在缺失或错位结构时才增加几何容量。三者协同决定优化哪些视图、用多少图像细节以及表示在哪里生长。

Result: 在 Replica、TUM RGB-D、ScanNet++ 及真实 RGB-D 传感器序列上评估，同时考察最终重建和采集过程中的地图质量。在 TUM RGB-D fr3/long_office_household 上，使用相同真值建图位姿时 EliGSiR 达 21.52 dB，优于 SplaTAM 的 19.42 dB；在跟踪位姿对比中，EliGSiR 结合实时 ORB-SLAM3 位姿在 155.5 s 内达到 23.02 dB，优于 CaRtGS 使用其原生跟踪器在 230.9 s 内达到的 20.10 dB。

Conclusion: EliGSiR 的自适应视图调度、监督保真度与几何增长机制能够显著改善有限建图预算的利用效率，在速度与重建质量上均优于现有持续建图基线，验证了按地图状态动态调配资源这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Conventional 3D Gaussian Splatting assumes a closed set of observations and long optimization schedules. Continual RGB-D mapping in contrast poses the problem that new observations arrive online, while previously reconstructed regions must be preserved. We present EliGSiR (Evidence-guided Load-adaptive Incremental Gaussian Splatting with Image Replay), a continual Gaussian mapper that controls how the available optimization budget is used as the reconstruction evolves. Map-Guided View Scheduling filters redundant incoming views and reconsiders retained views according to the current state of the map. Load-Adaptive Fidelity adjusts supervision resolution to the current mapping load instead of following a fixed resolution schedule. Targeted Geometry Growth separates depth supervision from Gaussian creation and adds geometric capacity only where repeated RGB-D observations indicate missing or misplaced structure. Together, these mechanisms adapt which views are optimized, how much image detail is used, and where the representation grows while mapping remains active. We evaluate EliGSiR on Replica, TUM RGB-D, ScanNet++, and real RGB-D sensor sequences, considering both the final reconstruction and the map available throughout acquisition. On TUM RGB-D fr3/long_office_household, EliGSiR reaches 21.52 dB with the same ground-truth mapping poses used by the controlled baselines, compared with 19.42 dB for SplaTAM. In the tracked-pose comparison, EliGSiR with live ORB-SLAM3 poses reaches 23.02 dB in 155.5 s, compared with 20.10 dB in 230.9 s for CaRtGS using its native tracker. We further evaluate reconstruction throughout acquisition and show how EliGSiR adaptive view scheduling, supervision fidelity, and geometry growth improve the use of the available mapping budget.

</details>


### [78] [MM-Future: Multi-Mode Joint World-Action Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20377)
*Shuai Liu,Hechangle Gong,Hao Jiang,Runlin He,Junxiang Zhan,Kai Huang,Sheng Yang,Shaoqing Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-Future，一种用于自动驾驶的多模态世界-动作联合模型，同时生成多组配对场景与动作假设，并通过模态感知扩散Transformer实现场景与动作的协同演化，在NAVSIM navtest上取得94.0 PDMS和91.5 EPDMS，在HUGSIM零样本闭环评估中达到32.3 HD-Score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶面临在多重不确定性下的决策与场景演化耦合问题，现有方法通常将世界模型与动作模型分离，未充分捕获二者间的双向交互以及多模态不确定性。

Method: MM-Future采用世界-动作模型框架：1) 生成多组配对场景-动作假设，从结构化动作先验和独立未来场景源初始化；2) 通过模态感知扩散Transformer实现每个假设对内的双向协同演化；3) 将多视角视频压缩为规划导向表示MM-Tokens以支持高效多模态展开；4) 使用未来条件化的提议评分器，基于共享历史上下文与配对预测未来对轨迹候选排序。

Result: 在NAVSIM navtest上达到94.0 PDMS和91.5 EPDMS，在HUGSIM零样本闭环评估中取得32.3 HD-Score。消融实验表明多模态联合世界-动作建模相比单模态和仅动作变体有一致性能提升。

Conclusion: MM-Future验证了多模态联合世界-动作建模的有效性，通过同时建模场景与动作的双向交互并压缩视频为规划导向表示，显著提升了自动驾驶预测与规划性能。

Abstract: Autonomous driving involves coupled decision-making and scene evolution under multi-mode uncertainty. To capture this coupling and uncertainty, we introduce MM-Future, a world-action model that generates multiple paired scene-action hypotheses and models bidirectional interaction within each pair. Each hypothesis is initialized from a structured action prior and an independent future scene source, which are then co-evolved through a modality-aware diffusion Transformer. To support efficient multi-mode rollout, MM-Future compresses multi-view video into planning-oriented representations, dubbed MM-Tokens. Finally, a future-conditioned proposal scorer ranks trajectory candidates by shared history context and their paired predicted future. On NAVSIM navtest, MM-Future achieves 94.0 PDMS and 91.5 EPDMS, while attaining a 32.3 HD-Score in zero-shot closed-loop evaluation on HUGSIM. Ablations show consistent improvements over both single-mode and action-only variants, validating the benefit of multi-mode joint world-action modeling.

</details>


### [79] [Compact Vision Models for Iris Presentation Attack Detection under Presentation Attack Instrument Shift and Environmental Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20386)
*Athanasios Angelakis,Marta Gomez-Barrero*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在LivDet-Iris 2017的Notre Dame子集上，对三种参数量不超过约26万的可训练紧凑型计算机视觉模型（含ZACH-ViT、Compact-TransMIL等）在呈现攻击仪器（PAI）导致的域偏移和环境退化下进行了鲁棒性评估。模型均无外部预训练和数据增强，训练5个种子，并将验证集选定阈值原样迁移到已知攻击、未知攻击、退化及合并测试分区。结果显示，从已知攻击到未知攻击，APCER上升17.11-30.47个百分点，D-EER上升7.38-12.73个百分点；ZACH-ViT在未知攻击下取得最低APCER（47.69±4.84%）和D-EER（38.87±0.93%），Compact-TransMIL取得最低BPCER。尽管最佳模型有相对优势，但绝对值误差较高，说明其尚不具备在未知PAI条件下的部署就绪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虹膜呈现攻击检测是安全关键任务，但在开发阶段表现可靠的子系统，当遇到验证数据中不存在的PAI或采集条件时可能失效。作者旨在量化紧凑型模型在PAI驱动的域偏移和环境退化下的真实鲁棒性，检验其是否为部署就绪。

Method: 对三种参数量均≤约26万的可训练参数的紧凑型scratch-trained计算机视觉模型进行基准测试，模型无外部预训练、无数据增强，每个模型用五个随机种子训练。在LivDet-Iris 2017的Notre Dame子集上，将验证集选定的阈值原样迁移（不调整）到已知攻击、未知攻击、退化和合并的测试分区中进行评估，记录APCER、D-EER、BPCER等指标。

Result: 从已知到未知攻击呈现，三模型的APCER增加17.11-30.47个百分点，D-EER增加7.38-12.73个百分点。在验证选定阈值下，ZACH-ViT获得最低未知攻击APCER（47.69±4.84%）和D-EER（38.87±0.93%），Compact-TransMIL获得最低BPCER；在APCER限值10%下ZACH-ViT仍取得最低未知攻击BPCER（81.29±1.95%）。

Conclusion: 即使是最佳紧凑模型的相对优势，也不足以构成其在未知PAI条件下的部署就绪性——高绝对错误率表明这些紧凑模型尚无法在遇到未见过的呈现攻击时安全部署，需要进一步提升泛化能力或引入其他缓解手段。

Abstract: Iris presentation attack detection (PAD) is security-critical when a subsystem that appears reliable during development encounters presentation attack instruments (PAIs) or acquisition conditions absent from validation data. We benchmark three compact scratch-trained computer-vision models, each with at most approximately 0.26 million trainable parameters, on the Notre Dame subset of LivDet-Iris 2017 under PAI-driven domain shift and environmental degradation. All models are trained without external pretraining or data augmentation and evaluated over five seeds. A validation-selected threshold is transferred unchanged to the known-attack, unknown-attack, corrupted, and pooled test partitions. From known to unknown attack presentations, Attack Presentation Classification Error Rate (APCER) increases by 17.11-30.47 percentage points and Detection Equal Error Rate (D-EER) increases by 7.38-12.73 percentage points. At the validation-selected threshold, ZACH-ViT obtains the lowest unknown-attack APCER (47.69 +/- 4.84%) and D-EER (38.87 +/- 0.93%), while Compact-TransMIL obtains the lowest Bona Fide Presentation Classification Error Rate (BPCER). ZACH-ViT also gives the lowest unknown-attack BPCER at an APCER limit of 10% (81.29 +/- 1.95%). The high absolute errors show that the comparative advantage of the best compact model does not constitute deployment readiness under unknown PAIs.

</details>


### [80] [TouchSight: Bare-Handed Tactile Prediction from Egocentric Video via Generative Visual Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20414)
*Danyan Zhou,Jinxuan Lu,Jiawei Lin,Tianxing Chen,Chuqiao Lyu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TouchSight 提出了一种基于单目第一人称（egocentric）视觉的密集全手接触力预测框架，利用500小时压力手套录制数据与丰富的双手交互（HOI）数据，并通过生成式视频模型构建 TwinTouch-20H 配对数据集以弥合手套与裸手之间的外观差异，实现了无需触觉传感器即可从视觉中恢复稠密触觉信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉信号对于理解物理交互和实现灵巧机器人操作至关重要，但由于触觉感知需要在接触界面上直接测量，大规模数据采集往往依赖侵入性、昂贵且受限的仪器，难以规模化推广。

Method: 1) 利用500小时压力手套录制数据及大量手-物交互（HOI）数据作为监督信号；2) 构建 TwinTouch-20H：20小时的配对视觉数据，使用生成式视频模型将手套录制的画面重新渲染为裸手观测并配以新背景，同时保留原始测量到的触觉标签，从而弥合手套训练数据与真实裸手场景之间的外观差距；3) TouchSight 网络从戴手套和生成的裸手视频中预测稠密接触力分布。

Result: TouchSight 在 OakInk2 基准上优于现有接触预测方法；在来自未见数据集的自然裸手第一人称视频上展现出定性泛化能力；随着手套监督数据规模的扩大，性能持续稳定提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，仅凭第一人称视觉即可恢复稠密触觉信号，无需在采集时部署任何触觉仪器，验证了视觉与触觉跨模态映射的可行性。

Abstract: Tactile signals provide direct contact and force measurements that are essential for understanding physical interactions and enabling dexterous robotic manipulation. However, tactile sensing requires direct measurement at contact interfaces, making large-scale data collection reliant on intrusive, costly, and restrictive instrumentation. We present TouchSight, a monocular egocentric vision framework for dense full-hand contact force prediction that leverages 500 hours of pressure-glove recordings and extensive hand-object interaction (HOI) data. To address the appearance gap between gloved training data and bare-hand real-world scenarios, we construct TwinTouch-20H: 20 hours of paired visual data in which generative video models re-render gloved recordings as bare-hand observations against new backgrounds while preserving the original measured tactile labels. TouchSight predicts dense force from both gloved and generated bare-hand videos, outperforms prior contact prediction methods on OakInk2, qualitatively generalizes to natural bare-hand egocentric videos from unseen datasets, and improves consistently as glove supervision scales. These results demonstrate that dense tactile signals can be recovered from egocentric vision alone, without tactile instrumentation at capture time.

</details>


### [81] [WeVisDoc: From Coverage to Capability for Robust End-to-End Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20423)
*Hao Yu,Kang Liu,Linnan Zhao,Jiabo Zhan,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WeVisDoc，一个两阶段数据驱动的文档解析框架。第一阶段通过异构数据与结构保留的退化合成拓宽语义、结构和外观覆盖；第二阶段利用留出探针在固定视觉-结构聚类内测量残差错误，指导定向数据构建与目标词元预算重分配。WeVisDoc-4B在OmniDocBench v1.6上取得95.38的Overall分数，并在PureDocBench三个赛道平均取得75.54，在所有四个设置中均位列端到端解析器第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析训练语料偏向常见文档类型和干净的数字页面，导致模型在多样化的布局与采集条件下性能不佳。然而仅扩大数据覆盖并不能明确如何解决解析器剩余的弱点，因此需要一种数据驱动方法，既能拓宽覆盖，又能精准定位并修复模型的薄弱环节。

Method: 提出两阶段数据驱动框架WeVisDoc。第一阶段通过异构数据增强和结构保留的退化合成，扩大语义、结构与外观多样性覆盖。第二阶段使用留出探针，在固定的视觉-结构聚类内度量第一阶段解析器的残差错误，依据这些诊断结果进行针对性数据构建，并对目标词元预算进行重分配，从而精准弥补第一阶段遗留的缺陷。

Result: WeVisDoc-4B在OmniDocBench v1.6上取得Overall分数95.38，在PureDocBench三个赛道平均Overall分数75.54，在全部四个设置中均位列端到端解析器第一。与第一阶段相比，第二阶段使2B与4B模型在两个基准上的Overall分数均获得提升，且在退化程度更高的PureDocBench赛道上增益更大，其中4B模型在Real Degraded赛道提升了4.03分。

Conclusion: 两阶段数据驱动框架能够有效提升文档解析的鲁棒性：第一阶段系统化拓宽数据覆盖，第二阶段通过诊断驱动的定向数据构建与预算重分配精准修复残差错误。该方法在多个基准上持续超越现有端到端解析器，尤其在退化与复杂场景下增益显著，验证了数据-centric策略对文档解析的可行性与价值。

Abstract: Document parsing converts document images into structured content and requires reliable performance across diverse layouts and acquisition conditions. Yet training corpora are biased toward common document types and clean digital pages, while expanding coverage alone does not specify how to address a parser's remaining weaknesses. We present WeVisDoc, a two-stage data-centric framework for robust end-to-end document parsing. Stage I broadens semantic, structural, and appearance coverage through heterogeneous data and structure-preserving degradation synthesis. Stage II uses a held-out probe to measure the Stage I parser's residual errors within fixed visual-structural clusters. These diagnostics guide targeted data construction and reallocation of the target-token budget. WeVisDoc-4B achieves an Overall score of 95.38 on OmniDocBench v1.6 and a mean Overall score of 75.54 across the three PureDocBench tracks, ranking first among the compared end-to-end parsers in all four settings. Compared with Stage I, Stage II improves Overall scores for the 2B and 4B models on both benchmarks, with larger gains on the degraded PureDocBench tracks, including a 4.03-point gain for the 4B model on the Real Degraded track.

</details>


### [82] [When Do Language-Grounded Explanations Help? A Graph-Bottleneck for Farm Monitoring Interpretable Sheep Facial Pain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20427)
*Alam Noor,Miguel Guti'errez Gait'an*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究绵羊面部表情的自动疼痛识别。作者将模型基于绵羊疼痛面部表情量表（SPFES），让每个检测到的面部区域对临床描述符的文本嵌入进行注意力操作，以生成可解释性依据，但测试发现这些解释并无实际意义（消融单个描述符仅改变约10^-4的logit，最受关注线索与预测疼痛等级的一致性仅32.6%）。因此作者移除"外观旁路"，改用概念瓶颈架构——分类器只读取SPFES概念分数，并以按区域标注的状态真值进行监督。尽管Cohen's κ下降0.05-0.10，但概念确被实证习得：少数疼痛指示状态以3.5-8.3倍的基准率被恢复，耳和眼的严重度排序在无严重度监督下自然涌现。仅移除监督则κ不变但概念准确率降至0.109，表明架构必要性不等于语义有效性。作者还指出在临床类别不平衡下，池化概念准确率具有误导性，并提供了七种方法在此数据集上的交叉验证、协议匹配的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从面部表情自动识别疼痛可使绵羊的连续福利评估变得实用，但实用化取决于信任——饲养员无法依据一个没有合理解释的评分采取行动。因此需要构建既可靠又可解释的疼痛识别模型。

Method: 初始方法基于SPFES：让每个检测到的面部区域对临床描述符的文本嵌入进行注意力操作以生成解释。发现解释无效后，采用概念瓶颈（concept bottleneck）架构，其中分类器仅读取SPFES概念分数，并用按区域标注的状态真值监督；同时评估消除监督的效果，并提出在临床类别不平衡下更可靠的评估方式。

Result: 基于注意力/文本嵌入的原始解释无效（消融影响约10^-4，一致性仅32.6%）。概念瓶颈虽使Cohen's κ下降0.05-0.10，但概念被实证习得：少数疼痛指示状态以3.5-8.3倍基准率恢复，耳/眼严重度排序自然涌现；仅移除监督则κ不变但概念准确率降至0.109。池化概念准确率在临床不平衡下具有误导性。

Conclusion: 架构上的必要性（如概念瓶颈的约束）并不自动带来语义有效性——概念必须通过细粒度、按区域的状态监督才能真正被学会。研究强调了在临床不平衡下评估概念质量时，池化准确率的局限性，并提供了一个七方法交叉验证的协议匹配基准。

Abstract: Automated pain recognition from facial expression could make continuous welfare assessment practical in sheep, but adoption depends on trust: a stockperson cannot act on a score that arrives without justification. We ground a model in the Sheep Pain Facial Expression Scale (SPFES) by letting each detected facial region attend over text embeddings of the clinical descriptors and then test whether the resulting explanations mean anything. They do not. Ablating an entire descriptor changes the predicted logit by about $10^{-4}$, and the most-attended cue agrees with the predicted pain level in only $32.6\%$ of regions, although the attention maps, the learned gate, and the generated text all proposed otherwise. We therefore remove the appearance bypass with a concept bottleneck whose classifier reads only SPFES concept scores, supervised by per-region state annotations that image-level pipelines discard. This costs $0.05$--$0.10$ in Cohen's $κ$ but yields concepts that are demonstrably learned: minority pain-indicating states are recovered at $3.5$--$8.3\times$ their base rates, and the ear and eye severity orderings emerge without severity supervision. Removing the supervision alone leaves $κ$ unchanged while concept accuracy falls to $0.109$, showing that architectural necessity does not imply semantic validity. We also show that pooled concept accuracy is misleading under clinical imbalance and provide a cross-validated, protocol-matched benchmark of seven methods on this dataset.

</details>


### [83] [Cross-Architecture Foundation-Model Distillation for Edge Flood Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20441)
*Fabian Schmalstieg,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过知识蒸馏将3亿参数的Prithvi-EO-2.0洪水分割教师模型压缩为70万参数的EfficientViT-B0学生模型。教师利用未标注的Sentinel-2影像监督学生训练，将252个手工标注场景的标注预算扩大至2500个场景的训练集。压缩后的INT8学生模型在Jetson Xavier NX上以5.57ms/512×512图像的速度运行，仅需约14MB运行时内存（1.5MB引擎文件），性能接近其教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球空间基础模型在洪水分割任务上性能优异，但其庞大的参数量限制了在内存受限的边缘硬件上的部署。作者旨在解决大模型难以部署到边缘设备的问题，同时探索如何用有限的标注预算训练一个紧凑但性能接近大模型的学生网络——即通过教师模型的监督来放大有限的人工标注预算。

Method: 采用教师-学生知识蒸馏框架：以在Sen1Floods11的252个手工标注训练场景上微调的3亿参数Prithvi-EO-2.0为教师，向0.7M参数的EfficientViT-B0学生传授知识。教师的监督信号使额外未标注的Sentinel-2影像也能用于训练，无需新的人工标注。作者进行了对照组实验（几何匹配的标签源控制）以隔离标签来源的影响，并比较了252与2500个场景两种数据规模。部署方面采用激活替换与量化感知训练，转化为INT8 TensorRT引擎。

Result: 在252场景的匹配预算下，教师监督训练与直接训练表现相当，并改善了STURM-Flood测试集上的表现；几何匹配对照表明标签来源本身不能完全解释差异。将教师监督数据池扩至2500场景后，float学生模型在Sen1Floods11测试集达到0.787的水体IoU（教师为0.822），在STURM-Flood上与教师持平，在WorldFloods-v2上仍低于教师。部署上，INT8学生模型在Jetson Xavier NX上每张512×512图像仅需5.57ms GPU计算时间、约14MB运行时设备内存。

Conclusion: 基础模型的监督可以将固定的人工标注预算放大为规模大得多的训练集，从而在保持边缘部署能力的前提下显著缩小学生-教师之间的性能差距，得到紧凑可部署的边缘模型。此外，固定MNDWI阈值在干净的外部基准上与两种模型相当，说明这些外部基准更适合作为泛化测试而非学习模型优于光谱规则证据。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models can provide strong flood-segmentation performance, but their size limits deployment on memory-constrained edge hardware. We distill a 300-million-parameter Prithvi-EO-2.0 teacher, fine-tuned on the 252 manually labeled Sen1Floods11 training scenes, into a 0.7-million-parameter EfficientViT-B0 student. The teacher supervises additional unlabeled Sentinel-2 imagery, allowing the student training set to grow without new manual annotations. At the matched budget of 252 scenes, teacher-supervised training is competitive with direct training and improves STURM-Flood performance across tested configurations; a geometry-matched control shows that label source alone does not explain the difference. Scaling the teacher-supervised pool to 2,500 scenes narrows the remaining student--teacher gap: the float student reaches 0.787 water intersection over union on the Sen1Floods11 test split against 0.822 for the teacher, matches the teacher on STURM-Flood under our evaluation protocol, and remains below it on WorldFloods-v2. After activation replacement and quantization-aware training, the student runs as a 1.5-megabyte 8-bit integer (INT8) TensorRT engine on a Jetson Xavier NX at 5.57 milliseconds of graphics processing unit (GPU) compute per 512-by-512 image, with approximately 14 megabytes of runtime device memory. A fixed modified normalized difference water index (MNDWI) threshold is competitive with both models on the two clean external benchmarks, so we interpret those benchmarks as generalization tests rather than as evidence of learned-model superiority over a spectral rule. The results support the conclusion: foundation-model supervision can amplify a fixed manual annotation budget into a substantially larger training set and yield a compact, deployable edge model.

</details>


### [84] [SenseFuse: Label-Free Fusion of Image and Shape Encoders for Open-Vocabulary 3D Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20475)
*Euiseok Han,Tri Ton,Hwanhee Kim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseFuse是一种无标签融合方法，通过平衡2D图像和3D形状编码器来进行开放词汇3D实例分割。该方法仅改进现有流程的掩码标注阶段，利用2D和3D编码器失败模式不重叠的特性实现互补，并通过自适应场景级融合权重（基于无标签敏感性度量）在毫秒级时间内从单场景未标注提案中估计权重。在ScanNet200、Replica和ScanNet++上所有设置均提升标注准确率，恢复67-100%（中位数93%）的oracle权重可获得增益，并在22项设置中21项提升实例AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇场景理解对机器人至关重要，但现有的开放词汇3D实例分割方法在掩码标注阶段仍主要局限于2D图像特征或图像蒸馏表示，未能充分利用3D形状信息。作者希望探索如何将3D形状信息融入标注环节以提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出SenseFuse，一个无标签融合方法：1) 揭示2D图像与3D形状编码器的失败模式基本不重叠、很少共享相同错误标签，而两个2D图像编码器常重复相同错误，因此2D+3D编码器对天然互补；2) 引入自适应机制，选择场景级融合权重以最大化无标签敏感性度量，该度量可直接从单场景未标注提案中在毫秒级估算；仅改进现有流程的掩码标注阶段。

Result: SenseFuse在ScanNet200、Replica和ScanNet++的所有评估设置中均提升标注准确率，恢复oracle权重可获得增益的67-100%（中位数93%）；在22项报告中21项提升实例AP。

Conclusion: 2D图像与3D形状编码器的失败模式互补性是开放词汇3D实例分割中有效的无标签融合依据，SenseFuse通过轻量自适应权重估计在无需标注的情况下显著提升标注和分割性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary scene understanding is fundamental for robotics, laying the groundwork for spatial reasoning and object manipulation. While closed-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation heavily leverages 3D shape information, state-of-the-art open-vocabulary methods remain predominantly restricted to 2D image features or image-distilled representations during mask labeling. In this paper, we propose SenseFuse, a label-free fusion method that balances 2D image and 3D shape encoders for robust open-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation, refining only the mask-labeling stage of existing pipelines. We reveal that 2D image and 3D shape encoders exhibit largely disjoint failure patterns and rarely share identical wrong labels, whereas two 2D image encoders frequently repeat the same errors. This distinct behavior makes the 2D and 3D pair inherently complementary. We introduce an adaptive mechanism that selects a scene-level fusion weight to maximize a label-free sensitivity measure, estimated directly from a single scene's unlabeled proposals in milliseconds. SenseFuse improves labeling accuracy in every evaluated setting across ScanNet200, Replica, and ScanNet++, recovering 67-100% (median 93%) of the gain achievable with an oracle weight, and it raises instance AP in 21 of 22 reported settings. Code is available at https://github.com/hanes1207/SenseFuse.

</details>


### [85] [Grounded Product Understanding in Livestream Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20508)
*Xinyu Zhang,Junjie Chen,Jiawei Ge,Qianlong Li,Libin Ma,Baokun Pan,Yahui Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 GPUB，一个包含 3,000 个电商直播实例、31K+ 时尚产品目录的大规模基准，用于联合评估目标产品识别与支撑时刻定位的"锚定产品理解（GPrU）"任务。现有多模态模型在该任务上表现不佳（最高 Pair mAP@.3 仅 10.13%），作者进一步提出统一产品理解模型 UniPro，通过共享多模态编码获取产品对齐且时间结构化的表征，将 Pair mAP@.3 提升至 21.53%，Joint R@1@.3 达 37.23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商直播作为在线展示产品的重要渠道，其视频中多个产品信息分散在不同时刻。现有的通用产品理解基准通常孤立地评估产品检索和时序定位，缺少对产品身份与时间证据之间关键对应关系的评估，这阻碍了以产品为中心的直播剪辑等下游应用的发展。

Method: 提出大规模基准 GPUB，包含 3,000 个直播实例、质量控制的多时刻时间标注以及超过 31K 时尚产品的目录，支撑三个评估任务：主任务 GPrU（联合识别目标产品并定位其支撑时刻）以及两个互补子任务（产品检索与产品时刻定位）。同时开发了统一模型 UniPro，利用共享多模态编码构建产品对齐且具有时间结构的表征。

Result: 现有多模态模型评估显示 GPrU 极具挑战性，最佳基线仅达 10.13% Pair mAP@.3；UniPro 将 Pair mAP@.3 提升至 21.53%，并在 GPrU 上取得 37.23% 的 Joint R@1@.3。

Conclusion: GPUB 填补了产品理解基准在"产品身份-时间证据对应关系"评估上的空白，验证了联合建模产品与时间定位的难度与必要性；UniPro 展示了共享多模态编码与时间结构化表征在弥合该性能差距上的有效性。

Abstract: E-commerce livestreams have emerged as an important channel for presenting products to online consumers, containing multiple products whose information is scattered in different moments. This poses significant challenges for downstream product understanding applications, such as product-centric livestream clipping, where models need to identify the product and its relevant segments for information gathering. However, existing benchmarks for general product understanding typically evaluate product retrieval and temporal localization in isolation, leaving the critical correspondence between product identity and temporal evidence largely unassessed. To address this limitation, we introduce GPUB, a large-scale benchmark comprising 3,000 livestream instances with quality-controlled multi-moment temporal annotations and a catalog of over 31K fashion products. GPUB supports three evaluation tasks: the main task Grounded Product Understanding (GPrU) requires jointly identifying the target product and localizing its supporting moments from a livestream video and a candidate product set; Product Retrieval and Product Moment Localization serve as two complementary subtasks. Evaluation of existing multimodal models shows that GPrU remains highly challenging, with the best-performing baseline achieving only 10.13% Pair mAP@.3. To narrow the performance gap, we further develop UniPro, a unified product understanding model that derives product-aligned and temporally structured representations from shared multimodal encoding, improving Pair mAP@.3 to 21.53% while achieving 37.23% Joint R@1@.3 on GPrU.

</details>


### [86] [Automated Goldsmith's Mark Retrieval in Silverware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20509)
*Atmik Tiwari,Vincent Christlein,Mark Fichtner,Freya Gohlke,Birgit Schübel,Theresa Witting,Heike Zech,Mathias Zinnen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI辅助的黄金工匠印记检索流水线，结合印记定位（mark localization）与度量学习微调，系统比较了三种主干网络（ImageNet预训练ResNet-50、监督ViT-S/16、自监督DINOv2 ViT-S/14）与三种裁剪策略（无裁剪、人工ground-truth裁剪、学习式检测裁剪）的组合。最优配置为DINOv2 ViT-S/14+人工裁剪+度量学习微调，达到mAP 62.63%、Top-1准确率73.74%。实验表明自监督预训练与印记定位是最关键的两个因素，学习式裁剪无需推理时的真值标注即可恢复人工裁剪的大部分收益。作者公开了人工标注数据集、代码库及Web界面以支持数字人文领域的可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在艺术史研究中，工匠印记（goldsmith marks）对于文物的鉴定与断代至关重要，但专家需人工将查询印记与数百个已记录样本逐一比对，过程既繁琐又严重依赖专业知识。作者旨在用AI检索流水线替代这一耗时的人工流程，同时兼顾准确性与可部署性。

Method: 构建由两部分组成的检索流水线：(1) 印记定位模块；(2) 基于度量学习微调的检索模块。主干网络为三种：ImageNet预训练ResNet-50、监督ViT-S/16、自监督DINOv2 ViT-S/14。系统评估三种裁剪策略（无裁剪、人工ground-truth裁剪、学习式检测裁剪）与各主干网络的交互效应，并以mAP和Top-1准确率为评价指标。

Result: 最优组合为DINOv2 ViT-S/14+人工裁剪+度量学习微调，mAP达62.63%，Top-1准确率73.74%。实验揭示自监督预训练与印记定位为两大最有效因素；学习式裁剪在推理时不需人工真值标注，却恢复了人工裁剪带来的大部分性能提升。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练（DINOv2）与印记定位是提升印记检索性能的核心要素，学习式裁剪提供了兼具性能与实用性的折中方案。作者公开数据集、代码库并部署Web接口，为数字人文领域的可复现研究与实际采用奠定基础。

Abstract: For art historians, goldsmith marks play a critical role in the identification and dating of artifacts. In practice, experts must manually compare a query mark against hundreds of documented examples, a process that is both tedious and highly dependent on specialist knowledge. To address this, we present an AI-assisted retrieval pipeline that combines mark localization with metric-learning fine-tuning across three backbone architectures: an ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-50, a supervised ViT-S/16, and a self-supervised DINOv2 ViT-S/14. We conduct a systematic evaluation of cropping strategies, where we measure the impact of no cropping, manual ground-truth cropping, and learned detection-based cropping, and assess their interaction with each backbone. Our strongest configuration, DINOv2 ViT-S/14 with manual crop and metric-learning fine-tuning, achieves an mAP of 62.63% and a Top-1 accuracy of 73.74%. Our experiments show that self-supervised pretraining and mark localization are the two most impactful factors, with learned cropping recovering the majority of the gain from manual cropping without requiring ground-truth annotations at inference time. To enable reproducibility and adoption in the digital humanities, we release our manually annotated dataset and codebase, and deploy the system via a public web interface.

</details>


### [87] [A Dual-Stream Regulated Reconstruction and Segmentation Network with Hierarchical Artifact-Prior Modeling for Ultra-Low-Field Pediatric Neuroimaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20562)
*Bahram Jafrasteh,Leo Milecki,Qingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文面向 LISA 2026 挑战赛，提出一个统一框架，在单个推理流水线中同时完成超低场（0.064 T）小儿 MRI 的自动质量评估、图像增强与次皮层结构分割三个任务。该框架基于带双耦合流的 3D U-Net，先重建增强后的 uLF 体数据，再将原始与增强图像联合用于分割；通过引入覆盖目标结构外脑组织区域的辅助类来稳定边界，并利用基于伪影图的条件头从重建残差与冻结的分割特征预测七类伪影评分；针对密集标注稀缺的问题，采用从图谱到目标的微分同胚配准进行标签传播并正则化解剖重建。作者给出了三个任务的验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 0.064 T 超低场小儿 MRI 存在低信噪比、弱解剖边界和频繁伪影，严重制约了自动化质量评估、图像增强与多结构分割三个任务的性能。传统方法通常将三个任务分别处理，缺乏统一协作，且密集标注严重稀缺，因此需要一个将三者耦合在统一推理流水线中的框架，以提升低场成像下的鲁棒性与精度。

Method: 提出一个统一框架：1) 以 3D U-Net 为基础构建双耦合流网络，一路重建增强的 uLF 体数据，另一路结合原始与增强图像进行次皮层结构分割；2) 引入“目标结构外脑组织”辅助类别（源自全脑掩膜）以增强边界稳定性；3) 设计一种以伪影图为条件输入的“头”模块，从重建残差和冻结的分割特征预测七类伪影评分；4) 利用从图谱到目标的微分同胚配准进行标签传播，弥补密集标注不足，并对解剖重建施加正则化。

Result: 作者在验证集上对该框架的三项任务（质量评估、图像增强、结构分割）进行了评估并报告了相应结果，表明各任务可在单一流水线中协同完成。

Conclusion: 通过将重建、分割与伪影评估耦合在统一框架内，并借助图谱配准解决标注稀缺问题，该工作为超低场小儿 MRI 提供了端到端的联合解决方案，有望提升低场成像下多任务分析的可行性，为 LISA 2026 挑战赛打下基础。

Abstract: Automated quality assessment, enhancement, and segmentation of multiple structures in $0.064\,\mathrm{T}$ ultra-low-field pediatric MRI are limited by a low signal-to-noise ratio, weak anatomical boundaries, and frequent artifacts. We present a unified framework for the LISA 2026 Challenge that performs all three tasks together within one inference pipeline. A network with two coupled streams, built on a 3D U-Net, first reconstructs an enhanced uLF volume and then combines the original and enhanced images for subcortical segmentation. To improve boundary stability, we add an auxiliary class covering brain tissue outside the target structures, derived from whole brain masks. A head conditioned on an artifact graph predicts the seven artifact ratings from reconstruction residuals and frozen segmentation features. We address the scarcity of dense annotations using diffeomorphic registration from atlas to target for label propagation and to regularize anatomical reconstruction. We report validation results across all three tasks.

</details>


### [88] [DocAttriBench: Benchmarking Answer Grounding in Document Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20574)
*Luca De Grandis,Silvia Cappelletti,William Raccagni,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DocAttriBench（DAB），一个大规模、细粒度、元素级来源归属基准，用于文档视觉问答（Document VQA）中的答案定位。为构建DAB，作者提出基于掩码的困惑度推导归属方法（MAPPET），结合文档布局与语言建模识别对每个答案最具信息量的元素，并应用于多个现有Document VQA数据集，生成包含23.7万文档和29.6万问答对（含元素级定位标注）的基准。基准测试显示，尽管更大模型通常取得更高答案准确率，即便最强模型也常无法准确定位支撑元素，突显了接地式、可验证、可信文档VQA模型的发展需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档视觉问答中的答案接地（answer grounding）仍是开放挑战：多数基准缺乏接地标注或仅有低质量标签，而构建接地数据集仍需要高昂的人工成本。这一困境阻碍了可验证、可信赖的Document VQA模型的发展，亟需大规模、高质量、低成本构建的接地基准。

Method: 提出MAPPET（Mask-based Perplexity-Derived Attribution）方法：结合文档布局与语言建模，通过掩码候选元素后测量困惑度（perplexity）增幅，将答案归属到对模型置信度贡献最大的元素。将该方法应用于多个现有Document VQA数据集，自动生成带元素级接地标注的大规模基准DAB，包含23.7万文档和29.6万问答对，接地目标涵盖文本块、表格、图像等具体布局元素。

Result: 在DAB上对具备接地能力的多模态大语言模型进行基准测试，从答案准确率、归属准确率和整体答案质量三个维度评估。结果显示：更大模型通常取得更高答案准确率，但即便最强模型也常无法准确定位支撑元素，说明答案准确与接地定位之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: DAB提供了可扩展的接地基准，可用于开发接地式、可验证、可信赖的Document VQA模型，填补了文档VQA中接地评估的空白。数据集与代码已公开发布。

Abstract: Answer grounding in document visual question answering remains an open challenge: most benchmarks lack grounding annotations or provide limited-quality labels, while constructing grounded datasets still requires costly manual effort. We introduce DocAttriBench (DAB), a large-scale benchmark for fine-grained, element-level source attribution in Document VQA, grounding answers to specific layout elements such as text blocks, tables, and images. To build DAB, we propose a Mask-based Perplexity-Derived Attribution method (MAPPET) that combines document layout and language modeling to identify the most informative element for each answer. MAPPET measures the increase in perplexity after masking candidate elements and attributes the answer to the element contributing most to model confidence. Applying MAPPET to multiple existing Document VQA datasets yields DAB, with 237k documents and 296k question-answer pairs with element-level grounding. We benchmark grounding-capable multimodal LLMs on DAB, evaluating answer accuracy, attribution accuracy, and overall answer quality. Results show that while larger models generally achieve higher answer accuracy, even the strongest models often fail to localize the supporting elements. DAB provides a scalable benchmark for developing grounded, verifiable, and trustworthy Document VQA models. Dataset and code are available at https://aimagelab.github.io/DocAttriBench/.

</details>


### [89] [RawSLAM: Online HDR Gaussian SLAM from Linear Radiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20589)
*Marina Orozco González,Luis Merino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了据作者所知首个在线高斯SLAM框架，可直接在单曝光16位线性HDR图像上进行跟踪与建图，无需离线Structure-from-Motion预处理。其核心包括三个组件：架构无关的HDR高斯泼溅模块（采用无MLP的对数参数化高斯颜色特征）、Reinhard范围压缩光度目标函数和结构引导的空间梯度加权。该方法在轨迹与重建精度上均优于直接HDR适配的MonoGS，且能以线性场景辐射度原生渲染；同一公式在标准8位输入上运行时可将MonoGS基线误差约减半；其HDR高斯模块可无缝迁移至SplaTAM、Gaussian SLAM和DROID-W，消除了这些系统在困难照明序列上的所有跟踪失败。作者还发布了包含10个真实室内序列（16位RAW图像、对齐深度、IMU和OptiTrack位姿）的RawSLAM数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密视觉SLAM几乎完全依赖8位色调映射的低动态范围（LDR）输入，在极端光照（阴影与高光）下容易发生跟踪漂移和建图崩溃；而现有的原始/HDR重建管线仅能离线运行，依赖SfM预处理且不适合大帧间运动。因此亟需一个能直接在HDR图像上在线跟踪与建图的SLAM框架。

Method: 方法基于三个核心组件：(1) 架构无关的HDR高斯泼溅模块，采用无MLP的对数参数化高斯颜色特征；(2) Reinhard范围压缩光度目标函数；(3) 结构引导的空间梯度加权。这些组件使系统能直接在16位线性HDR图像上优化，并支持以线性场景辐射度原生渲染。

Result: 相比直接HDR适配的MonoGS，该方法在轨迹与重建精度上均有提升；在标准8位输入上可将MonoGS基线误差约减半；HDR高斯模块可无缝迁移至SplaTAM、Gaussian SLAM和DROID-W，消除了这些系统在困难照明序列上的所有跟踪失败。作者引入包含10个真实室内序列的RawSLAM数据集以支持研究。

Conclusion: 实验表明所提出的HDR高斯SLAM框架具备良好的鲁棒性、通用性与跨架构迁移能力，能显著提升极端光照下的跟踪与重建性能，同时兼容8位输入并支持线性辐射度后处理，为未来SLAM在HDR/RAW数据上的在线实时应用提供了基础。

Abstract: Current dense visual SLAM systems rely almost exclusively on 8-bit tonemapped Low Dynamic Range (LDR) inputs, limiting their robustness in extreme lighting where shadows and highlights trigger tracking drift and mapping collapse. Conversely, existing raw and High Dynamic Range (HDR) reconstruction pipelines operate strictly offline. They depend on Structure-from-Motion preprocessing and are not suited for large inter-frame motion. We present, to the best of our knowledge, the first online Gaussian SLAM framework that tracks and maps directly on single-exposure 16-bit linear HDR imagery. Our method rests on three core components: an architecture-agnostic HDR Gaussian Splatting module featuring an MLP-free logarithmic parameterization of Gaussian color features; a Reinhard range-compressed photometric objective; and structure-guided spatial gradient weighting. Combined, these components allow our approach to outperform a direct HDR adaptation of MonoGS in both trajectory and reconstruction accuracy, while rendering natively in linear scene radiance for post-rendering processing. The same formulation runs unchanged on standard 8-bit inputs, roughly halving the MonoGS baseline error. Furthermore, our HDR Gaussian module transfers seamlessly to SplaTAM, Gaussian SLAM, and DROID-W, eliminating all tracking failures these systems suffer on challenging illumination sequences. To enable this research, we introduce RawSLAM: a dataset of 10 real-world indoor sequences featuring 16-bit RAW imagery, aligned depth, IMU measurements, and external OptiTrack poses. Code and dataset will be made publicly available soon.

</details>


### [90] [PhGS: Post-Hoc Pruning and Refinement of Single-View Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20623)
*Rinto Yagawa,Han Cheng,Dieter Schmalstieg,Hideo Saito,Shohei Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对单视图前馈式3D高斯溅射（3DGS）生成模型的、与骨干网络无关的压缩流水线。该方法冻结基础模型，通过基于重要性评分的剪枝与可训练的轻量级循环精化模块相结合，对生成的高斯进行事后剪枝和迭代更新，在保持新视角渲染保真度的同时大幅降低内存占用，并支持推理时灵活的保留比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图前馈式3DGS生成方法通常为每条相机射线预测固定数量（过多）的高斯，引入严重的空间冗余。现有的大多数压缩策略针对多视图设置设计，利用跨视图一致性，因此无法直接应用于单图像模型。与其重新训练基础前馈网络以直接输出紧凑表示，作者希望保持基础模型冻结，转而进行事后压缩。

Method: 提出一种与骨干网络无关的单视图前馈3DGS压缩流水线，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于重要性评分的剪枝机制，用于筛选出具有较高贡献的存活基元（高斯）；(2) 一个可训练的轻量级循环精化模块，用于迭代更新存活的基元以恢复因剪枝而损失的图像质量。整个过程为事后（post-hoc）处理，无需改动或重训基础生成网络。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法能与现有基线无缝集成，在保持新视角渲染保真度的同时实现显著的内存压缩；并支持推理时灵活调整保留比例（keep ratios）以适应不同应用需求。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在冻结基础前馈模型的情况下，通过"剪枝+循环精化"的事后压缩路径能够有效消除单视图3DGS的空间冗余，兼具与既有方法的通用兼容性和推理时的灵活可控性，是一种低成本且高效的压缩方案。

Abstract: Recent single-view feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) generation predicts a fixed number of Gaussians per camera ray, introducing severe spatial redundancy. Most existing compaction strategies target multi-view setups to exploit cross-view consistency and are incompatible with single-image models. Instead of retraining the base feed-forward network to directly output compact representations, our insight is to keep the base models frozen and apply post-hoc pruning and recurrent refinement to the generated Gaussians. Consequently, we propose a backbone-agnostic compaction pipeline for single-view feed-forward 3DGS that couples an importance-score-based pruning mechanism with a trainable, lightweight recurrent refinement module, which iteratively updates the surviving primitives to restore image quality. Our results demonstrate seamless integration with existing baselines while preserving novel-view rendering fidelity and achieving high memory reduction. Furthermore, our method supports flexible inference-time keep ratios for application needs.

</details>


### [91] [Refinement Is Inherently Editable: Training-Free Prompt-to-Prompt Image Editing with Generative Refinement Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20633)
*Yulong Chen,Ziqian Zhang,Haoyu Zhang,Ao He,Senmao Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefineEdit 是一种基于生成式精化网络的免训练 prompt-to-prompt 图像编辑框架，通过全局精化二值图像编码来耦合编辑定位与内容生成，允许随图像演化重新评估编辑证据。它在 PIE-Bench 九个编辑类别上取得了最佳的后景保持分数以及最高的整体和编辑区域 CLIP 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散编辑器依赖空间控制，其不准确性可能导致编辑不完整或改动无关区域；而因果自回归编辑器受固定解码顺序限制，难以修正早期决策。作者旨在解决编辑定位不准确与解码顺序受限这两大痛点，实现既保留源内容又精确引入所需修改的编辑。

Method: RefineEdit 的核心理念是将编辑定位与内容生成通过全局精化二值图像编码耦合起来。具体做法：1) 从中间源状态初始化一个编辑分支并复用其形成的布局；2) 比较两个分支对同一源采样比特所赋予的概率，用其符号差来挑选可编辑的位置与比特；3) 选中比特遵循编辑精化，其余比特复制演化中的源状态；4) 通过自适应空间冻结限制不必要的掩码扩张，用有限比特锁定保持近期选中的比特可编辑。该方法无需额外训练、外部掩码或注意力控制。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 的九个编辑类别上，RefineEdit 在 PSNR、LPIPS、MSE 和 SSIM 上取得了最佳的后景保留分数，同时在被评估方法中获得了最高的整体图像和编辑区域 CLIP 分数。

Conclusion: RefineEdit 证明了通过全局二进制代码精化耦合编辑定位与内容生成，能够在无需训练或额外控制手段的情况下同时实现高精度的编辑效果和出色的源内容保持，为自回归图像编辑提供了一种有效的训练无关方案。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing must introduce the requested changes while preserving unrelated source content. Diffusion-based editors rely on spatial controls whose inaccuracies can leave edits incomplete or alter unrelated regions. Causal autoregressive editors face a further constraint: their fixed decoding order limits revision of earlier decisions. We introduce RefineEdit, a training-free prompt-to-prompt image editing framework built on a Generative Refinement Network. Our key idea is to couple edit localization with content generation through the global refinement of binary image codes, allowing editing evidence to be reassessed as the image evolves. RefineEdit initializes an editing branch from an intermediate source state, reusing the emerging layout. We compare the probabilities assigned by the two branches to the same source-sampled bits, using their signed differences to select editable positions and bits. Selected bits follow editing refinement, while the remaining bits copy the evolving source state. To stabilize editing across refinement steps, adaptive spatial freezing limits unnecessary mask expansion, while finite bit locking keeps recently selected bits editable. The framework requires no additional training, external masks, or attention control. Across nine editing categories of PIE-Bench, RefineEdit achieves the best background-preservation scores in PSNR, LPIPS, MSE and SSIM, together with the highest whole-image and edited-region CLIP scores among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [92] [PROVIA: Procedure State Tracking for Online Mistake Detection in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20638)
*Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Jimmy Weissert,Luc Maria Scherrer,Cedric Zöllner,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Jiale Wei,Junwei Zheng,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PROVIA是一个面向第一视角（egocentric）视频的在线程序错误检测系统。它区分两类记录：事实状态（对每个执行者已执行步骤的习得摘要，含错误）与已接受进度（基于正确演示通过贝叶斯状态合并归纳出的自动机状态上的精确后验，以及各执行者执行状态的精确后验）。仅在正确状态分支发生过程状态转移，错误与纠正分支保留源状态。通过序贯检验将每帧错误概率转化为警报。在CaptainCook4D、IndustReal、HoloAssist和IMPACT-ego数据集上，PROVIA在受控基线中排名最佳；在0.1次/分钟的验证误报预算下，Recall在CaptainCook4D上为0.154对0.128，在HoloAssist上为0.034对0.015。运行速度为58-70帧/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线错误检测方法采用“首次错误协议”（first-mistake protocol），在每个录制片段第一次出错处截断，这使得仅利用固定时间规则（从不看视频内容）的基线即能在所有案例上正确预测。作者指出现实场景中错误与恢复会自然发生，需要评估完整试验，因此在验证误报预算和纯时序控制基线之下进行评测，以证明方法真正利用视频内容而非时间伪迹。

Method: PROVIA维护两条独立记录：(1) 事实状态——对每位执行者已执行步骤（含错误）的习得摘要；(2) 已接受进度——由正确演示经贝叶斯状态合并归纳出的自动机状态上的精确后验，以及每位执行者执行状态的精确后验。过程状态转移仅发生在“正确状态”分支；错误与纠正分支保留源状态。采用一个序贯检验将每帧错误概率转化为告警，配合一个过滤器与一个优化规则。

Result: 在CaptainCook4D、IndustReal、HoloAssist和IMPACT-ego四个数据集上，PROVIA以单一过滤器与单一优化规则在受控基线中错误排序最佳。在0.1次假警报/分钟的验证预算下，CaptainCook4D上Recall为0.154对比0.128，HoloAssist上为0.034对比0.015，并在每个预算下均领先。管线运行速度为58-70帧/秒，源码已开源。

Conclusion: PROVIA通过分离事实状态与已接受进度，并利用贝叶斯状态合并构造的程序自动机后验，能够在完整试验中稳健检测程序性错误并处理恢复情境，显著优于仅依赖时序的基线，证明其确实基于视频内容推理而非时间规律，达到实时运行要求。

Abstract: An assistant watching egocentric video should notice a mistake from past frames alone, before the next step begins, and keep working once the person recovers. A mistake changes the state of the work, so every later step has to be read against what was done rather than against the plan. The first-mistake protocol that current online methods report on cuts each recording at its first mistake, so a fixed-time rule that never looks at the video is right on every case. We evaluate on complete trials, where mistakes and recoveries arise naturally, under a validation false-alarm budget and against controls that use timing alone. PROVIA keeps two records apart: a factual state, a learned summary of the steps each actor performed, mistakes included, and the accepted progress, an exact posterior over the state of an automaton induced from correct demonstrations by Bayesian state merging and over the execution status of each actor. Procedure-state transitions occur only in the correct-status branch; the mistake and correction branches retain the source state. A sequential test turns the per-frame mistake probability into alarms. With one filter and one optimization rule, PROVIA ranks mistakes best among the evaluated controlled baselines on CaptainCook4D, IndustReal, HoloAssist and IMPACT-ego. At a validation budget of 0.1 false alarms per minute it recalls .154 against .128 on CaptainCook4D and .034 against .015 on HoloAssist, where it leads at every budget. The pipeline runs at 58-70 frames per second. The source code is available at https://github.com/Kratos-Wen/PROVIA.

</details>


### [93] [Earth Surface Immune System for Rapid Monitoring of Unknown Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20662)
*Jingtao Li,Qian Zhu,Xinyu Wang,Deren Li,Liangpei Zhang,Yanfei Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ESIA（地球表面免疫系统）从生物免疫系统汲取设计灵感，构建了针对未知地表异常事件的双阶段检测-识别框架。非特异性先天免疫阶段以14.51 km²/s的速度生成二值定位图（相比最强通用基线F1提升37%）；特异性适应性免疫阶段通过负选择过滤文本提示，并借助多模态基础模型实现开放词汇的异常属性识别（F1>80%）；突变机制在测试时仅用单对参考图像即可在3.26秒内自适应微调嵌入。该框架在覆盖19,801.60 km²、六类异常、与22个模型对比的全球数据集上验证，并成功应用于第聂伯河三角洲农田退化量化与2025年洛杉矶帕利塞兹火灾燃烧严重度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化与人类活动导致的地表异常日益频发且多样化，但异常事件历史数据稀缺且不可预测，使其本质不同于常规遥感目标。现有方法或局限于特定异常类别，或停留在定位阶段，在"检测到"与"可行动信息"之间存在鸿沟。作者希望借鉴生物免疫系统经过数百万年进化形成的应对多样且不确定威胁的机制，构建一个无需预设类别、既能定位又能开放识别未知异常属性的统一框架。

Method: ESIA架构受生物免疫系统三条原则约束，设计为两个阶段加一个机制：(1) 非特异性先天免疫阶段——将异常视为时序卫星影像中未观测到的变化，在不假设任何异常类别的前提下生成二值定位图，处理速度14.51 km²/s；(2) 特异性适应性免疫阶段——应用负选择机制过滤文本提示，并将存活的提示与定位到的影像块通过多模态基础模型匹配，实现包括类别、受影响面积、损害严重度在内的未知异常属性开放词汇识别；(3) 突变机制——在测试时仅用单一参考图像对（3.26秒）微调最小嵌入向量，实现逐场景自适应。

Result: 在覆盖19,801.60 km²、六类异常、与22个模型对比的全球数据集上验证：先天免疫阶段定位F1较最强通用基线提升37%；适应性免疫阶段识别F1超过80%；突变机制单场景自适应仅需3.26秒。此外成功应用于Kakhovka大坝坍塌后第聂伯河三角洲退化农田的量化，以及2025年洛杉矶帕利塞兹火灾的燃烧严重度评估。

Conclusion: ESIA在无需预设类别条件下同时实现了未知地表异常的快速定位与开放词汇属性识别，其灵活性与自适应能力填补了检测与可行动信息之间的鸿沟，为实时灾害响应和环境监测开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Earth surface anomalies, driven by escalating climate change, and expanding human activities, are increasing in both frequency and diversity, yet their limited historical data and unpredictability make them fundamentally different from conventional remote sensing targets. Existing methods address specific anomaly categories or stop at localization, leaving a gap between detection and actionable information. Here we present ESIA, an Earth Surface Immune System whose architecture is constrained by three principles from the biological immune system, refined over millions of years against equally diverse and uncertain threats. A non-specific innate immune stage treats anomalies as unobserved changes in time-series satellite imagery, generating binary localization maps at 14.51 km2/s without assuming any anomaly category, surpassing the strongest general baseline by 37% in F1. A specific adaptive immune stage applies negative selection to filter text prompts and matches surviving prompts with localized image patches through a multi-modal foundation model, enabling open-vocabulary recognition of unknown anomaly attributes including category, affected area, and damage severity, with recognition F1 exceeding 80%. A mutation mechanism tunes minimal embeddings at test time, adapting to each scene in 3.26s using a single reference image pair. We validate ESIA on a global-scale dataset covering 19,801.60 km2 across six anomaly categories, comparing against 22 models, and further apply it to quantify degraded farmland in the Dnipro Delta following the Kakhovka Dam collapse and assess burn severity from 2025 Palisades Fire in Los Angeles. This unprecedented flexibility in handling unknown anomalies opens new avenues for real-time disaster response and environmental surveillance.

</details>


### [94] [FunArt: Decoding Functional Structure and Articulation from Generative 3D Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20673)
*Dennis Rotondi,Abdelrhman Werby,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FunArt框架，用于从静态单配置的RGB-D观测中构建具有可动结构感知的功能性3D场景图。FunArt重建物体实例，将融合几何直接转化为TRELLIS.2的O-Voxel表示，利用其冻结的稀疏压缩VAE作为结构先验，通过轻量级查询解码器联合完成可动部件分割、功能交互元素分割以及运动类型、轴、起点和范围估计。在Articulate3D数据集上，FunArt在可动部件分割、可动结构估计和功能元素分割方面均达到最先进性能，端到端设置下相比最强基线分别提升1.5、2.8和6.7个AP_50点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人类环境中有效运作需要识别可动结构物体（articulated objects），分割其可移动且可交互的部件，并估计其运动学模型。现有方法通常从观测到的交互中恢复运动学，或在使用静态扫描时将可动结构与功能交互元素解耦，缺乏能够从单一静态配置中同时恢复可动结构和功能交互元素的统一表示。

Method: FunArt构建可动结构感知的功能性3D场景图：首先从单次静态配置的RGB-D观测中重建物体实例；然后将融合几何直接转换为TRELLIS.2的O-Voxel表示，利用其冻结的稀疏压缩VAE作为结构先验；最后通过轻量级查询解码器，将紧凑的物体级隐向量与密集的表面对齐特征相结合，联合分割可动部件与功能交互元素，同时估计运动类型、轴、起点和范围。

Result: 在Articulate3D数据集上，FunArt在可动部件分割、可动结构估计和功能元素分割方面均达到SOTA水平，具备物体是否提供GT输入均有效。在端到端设置下，相比最强基线，可动部件AP_50提升1.5点，联合起止点与轴约束下提升2.8点，功能元素提升6.7个AP_50点。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明生成式3D隐向量编码了可操作的结构线索，这些线索可以在物理交互之前初始化机器人的感知与规划，说明无需依赖交互痕迹即可从静态场景中推断物体的可动结构与功能。

Abstract: To operate effectively in human environments, robots must identify articulated objects, segment their movable and interactive parts, and estimate their kinematic models. Existing articulated scene representations typically recover kinematics from observed interactions, while methods operating on static scans often decouple articulation from functional interactive elements. We present FunArt, a framework that constructs articulation-aware functional 3D scene graphs from posed RGB-D observations captured in a single static configuration. FunArt reconstructs object instances, converts their fused geometry directly into the O-Voxel representation of TRELLIS.2, and exploits its frozen, sparse-compression VAE as a structural prior. A lightweight query-based decoder combines compact object-level latents with dense, surface-aligned features to jointly segment movable parts and functional interactive elements while estimating motion type, axis, origin, and range. On the Articulate3D dataset, FunArt achieves state-of-the-art performance across movable-part segmentation, articulation estimation, and functional-element segmentation, both with and without ground-truth object input. In the end-to-end setting, it outperforms the strongest baselines by 1.5 AP_{50} points for movable parts, 2.8 AP_{50} points under joint origin-and-axis constraints, and 6.7 AP_{50} points for functional elements. These results demonstrate that generative 3D latents encode actionable structural cues that can initialize robotic perception and planning before physical interaction.

</details>


### [95] [Should This Case Be Adapted? Prediction Fragmentation Controls Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20700)
*Lili Wang,Jing Li,Xiaowen Sun,Xiangyu Hu,Zhuangzhuang Gu,Jian Liu,Srihari Nelakuditi,Yan Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对"间歇性测试时自适应"（Episodic test-time adaptation）中固定自适应步数带来的问题，指出固定视界（fixed horizon）混淆了"整批适配多大幅度"与"单个病例是否应适配"两类问题。由于全局固定预算无法按病例条件化，跨厂商心脏MRI中均值ΔDice虽近乎为零，但58.7%的病例被单方面损害。作者提出"预测碎片化"（prediction fragmentation）——即源模型$M_0$与适配掩码$M_k$之间的不一致几何——可在无需标签与额外反向传播的情况下预测有害接受面积（HA），并据此构建病例级路由（router），将三个基准上的HA显著降低（如心脏基准上从0.129降至0.013，并将58.7%的损害降至20.0%）。该模板可跨架构与领域移植（nnU-Net→SegFormer，Cityscapes→ACDC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有间歇性测试时自适应对每个病例重置到源权重并以固定步数适配。固定视界把"整批适配多远"（队列层问题）与"该病例究竟要不要适配"（不可约的病例层问题）混为一谈。由于全局预算无法按病例条件化，跨厂商心脏MRI上均值统计上不显著，但过半病例被单方面恶化，说明均值掩盖了决策层面的伤害，亟需一种无标签、无梯度的逐病例路由机制。

Method: 作者用"有害接受面积"（HA，即控制器部署的编辑区域中有害部分的比例）量化自适应造成的伤害。核心信号是"预测碎片化"：$M_0$与$M_k$之间预测不一致的分割几何，它可在决策时无需标签或额外反向传播来预测HA。基于此构建病例级路由器，仅在保留下来的病例上应用适配更新；阈值在独立的标注子集上拟合一次，决策阶段不使用标签或梯度。

Result: 预测碎片化在三个基准上对HA的预测与梯度范数相当（Spearman ρ 0.50–0.60），而延迟仅为后者的四分之一。在一项未参与设计、仅冻结设计并重新校准切点的基准上，路由器将HA从0.228降至0.139；在用于设计的（selected on）心脏基准上，HA从0.129降至0.013，同时保持Dice匹配、仅部署1.10次更新，优于事后在评估标签上寻优的回顾性最优预算，并将58.7%的受损病例降至20.0%（作为其上界进行了量化）。在前列腺等保留病例并非净获益的场合，路由器仍削减HA但会牺牲精度，该边界亦被报告。

Conclusion: 固定自适应视界在病例层面是有害的，而预测碎片化提供了一种廉价、无监督、无梯度的信号来逐例判断是否适配，所构造的路由器可显著降低伤害并跨架构、跨领域移植（nnU-Net→SegFormer，Cityscapes→ACDC），参数、阈值与分桶策略按领域实例化。

Abstract: Episodic test-time adaptation resets a frozen segmenter to source weights $M_0$ on each case and adapts for a fixed step count. A fixed horizon conflates a cohort-level question, how far to adapt, with an irreducibly per-case one, whether this case should be adapted at all. Cohort means hide that decision: on cross-vendor cardiac MRI the mean $Δ$Dice from adaptation is statistically indistinguishable from zero while 58.7% of cases are individually made worse. We quantify this harm as harmful accepted area (HA), the harmful fraction of the edited area a controller deploys. Held-out tuning gives a stronger baseline than a fixed horizon, but the budget it selects transfers on neither of the two main medical benchmarks, and no global budget can condition on the case.
