We show that prediction fragmentation---the disagreement geometry between $M_0$ and the adapted mask $M_k$---predicts HA with no labels or extra backward passes at decision time, comparably on three benchmarks (Spearman $ρ$ 0.50--0.60), at a quarter of gradient-norm's latency. A case-level router built on it cuts HA from 0.228 to 0.139 on a benchmark that took no part in its design, with the design frozen and only cut-points recalibrated there. On the cardiac benchmark the design was selected on, the router cuts HA from 0.129 to 0.013 at matched Dice and 1.10 deployed updates, against the retrospective-best budget found post hoc on evaluation labels, and reduces that 58.7% to 20.0%, an upper bound we quantify. Where the retained cases are not net-helped (as on prostate), the router still cuts HA but concedes accuracy, a boundary we report. Thresholds are fit once on a labeled split disjoint from evaluation; decisions use no labels or gradients. The template ports across architecture and domain (nnU-Net$\to$SegFormer, Cityscapes$\to$ACDC) with coordinate, thresholds and per-bucket actions instantiated per domain.

</details>


### [96] [FlowSGS: Improving Flow Matching Priors for Inverse Imaging with Stochastic Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20769)
*Tianao Li,Xinhui Qian,Emma Alexander*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowSGS，一种基于Flow Matching的流式后验采样方法，利用Split Gibbs Sampling (SGS)将后验分解为似然步和先验步。似然步使用Langevin动力学采样，先验步借助Stochastic Interpolants (SI)框架整合预训练的流模型。通过SI的逆向时间SDE和创新的时间步校正技术，FlowSGS在较少的网络评估下达到最先进的逆问题求解性能，并首次在非线性逆问题（傅里叶相位恢复）上进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配已成为最先进的生成模型，常作为即插即用(PnP)先验求解计算成像中的逆问题。然而现有基于流的逆求解器假设线性前向模型和/或在后验采样中做简化近似，限制了其适用范围和精度，因此需要一种能处理非线性问题且更精确的后验采样方法。

Method: 采用Split Gibbs Sampling (SGS)将后验分解为似然步和先验步；似然步利用Langevin动力学进行采样；先验步利用Stochastic Interpolants (SI)框架集成预训练流模型。给出基于SI逆向时间SDE的先验步形式，并利用流先验的直线概率路径及一种新的逆向时间SDE时间步校正技术，减少先验步中的网络评估次数。

Result: 实验表明FlowSGS在一系列逆问题上取得最先进的性能，并且首次为基于流的逆求解器提供了非线性逆问题（傅里叶相位恢复）的实验验证，同时相比即插即用扩散采样器所需网络评估次数更少。

Conclusion: FlowSGS通过SGS整合流模型先验与Langevin似然采样，克服了现有基于流的逆求解器只能处理线性模型和简化近似的局限，在效率和性能上均优于既有方法，并拓展了流式逆求解器到非线性逆问题的能力。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as the state-of-the-art generative model and has been used for plug-and-play (PnP) priors to solve inverse problems in computational imaging. However, existing flow-based inverse solvers assume linear forward models and/or make simplifying approximations in posterior sampling. To circumvent these problems, we introduce FlowSGS, a flow-based posterior sampling method using Split Gibbs Sampling (SGS) to decompose the posterior into a likelihood step and a prior step. Specifically, we sample from the likelihood step using Langevin dynamics and leverage the Stochastic Interpolants (SI) framework to integrate a pretrained flow model into the prior step. We provide a form for the prior step that uses SI's reverse-time SDE, and show connections to previous PnP methods. Moreover, with the aid of the flow prior's straight probability paths and a novel timestep correction technique for the reverse-time SDE, FlowSGS requires fewer network evaluations in its prior step than plug-and-play diffusion samplers. Our experiments show state-of-the-art performance on a range of inverse problems. For the first time, we provide an experiment on a nonlinear inverse problem (Fourier phase retrieval) for flow-based inverse solvers.

</details>


### [97] [ERCPMP-Gx: Endoscopic Image and Video Dataset for Morphological, Histopathological, and Genomic Characterization of Colorectal Polyposis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20815)
*Zahra Ghaffari,Massih Bahar,Mojgan Forootan,Ali Darvishi,Hamidreza Bolhasani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍ERCPMP-Gx，一个将内镜、病理和基因组数据整合于患者层面的结直肠息肉病AI数据集。该数据集包含160张图像及视频片段，约80%为经临床或遗传学确诊的遗传性息肉病综合征（FAP、PJS、JPS、GNS），20%为非遗传性息肉及形态相似的模仿病变，用于支持鉴别分类。每条记录关联标准化内镜标注、代表性组织病理学和临床报告的胚系发现，形成AI就绪、患者层面的注释框架，公开于Mendeley。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传性息肉病综合征是结直肠癌的前驱病变，伴随广泛的结肠外肿瘤。早期识别与准确分类对及时诊断、个体化患者管理和针对性家庭监测至关重要。然而，现有公共内镜数据集多围绕单个散发性息肉组织，缺乏在患者层面将息肉病表型与组织病理学和胚系发现相关联的数据集，难以支撑AI在该领域的应用。

Method: 采用奥林巴斯EVIS X1系统进行内镜检查，结合白光内镜（WLE）、窄带成像（NBI）、放大NBI（M-NBI）及近聚焦NBI模式，采集160张图像及视频片段。数据集按病因分为遗传性息肉病组（约80%，含FAP、PJS、JPS、GNS）和非遗传性息肉/类息肉模仿病变组（约20%，用于鉴别分类）。每条记录关联标准化内镜注释、代表性组织病理学和临床报告的胚系发现，构建患者层面的AI就绪注释框架。

Result: 构建了ERCPMP-Gx多模态数据集，整合内镜图像/视频、组织病理学和胚系基因组数据于患者层面，覆盖遗传性与非遗传性息肉病及其模仿病变，支持AI在息肉病识别、表征和分类中的应用。数据集已在Mendeley公开（DOI: 10.17632/nzyfc544bx.2），并配套DataBioX网站提供更新信息。

Conclusion: ERCPMP-Gx填补了公共内镜数据集在患者层面关联息肉病表型、病理与胚系发现的空白，为AI辅助遗传性息肉病综合征的识别和分类提供了数据基础，有助于及时诊断、个体化管理及家族监测。

Abstract: Hereditary polyposis syndromes can be precursor lesions to colorectal cancer and are associated with a broad spectrum of extracolonic tumors. Early identification and accurate classification of these syndromes are essential for timely diagnosis, individualized patient management, and targeted surveillance strategies for affected families. However, public endoscopic datasets are largely organized around the individual sporadic polyp, and none links the polyposis phenotype to histopathology and germline findings at the patient level. Here, we present ERCPMP-Gx, an endoscopic, histopathological, and genomic dataset developed to support the application of artificial intelligence (AI) in the recognition, characterization, and classification of colorectal polyposis. Most procedures were performed using the Olympus EVIS X1 system with white-light endoscopy (WLE), narrow-band imaging (NBI), magnifying NBI (M-NBI), and NBI with near focus modes, yielding 160 images and accompanying video clips. Approximately eighty percent of cases represent clinically and/or genetically confirmed hereditary polyposis syndromes (PG), including familial adenomatous polyposis (FAP), Peutz-Jeghers syndrome (PJS), juvenile polyposis syndrome (JPS), and ganglioneuroma syndrome (GNS), while the remaining twenty percent comprise non-hereditary polyps and polyp-mimicking lesions with overlapping morphological features (Non-PG), included to support differential classification. Each released record is linked, where available, to standardized endoscopic annotations, representative histopathology, and clinically reported germline findings, forming an AI-ready, patient-level annotation framework. The dataset is publicly accessible at Mendeley (https://doi.org/10.17632/nzyfc544bx.2). For the latest updates and further information, readers are referred to the DataBioX website: https://databiox.com.

</details>


### [98] [Paint-Anything: Unified Any-Color Control for Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20816)
*Ji Xie,Dewei Zhou,Xinyu Huang,Zhennan Chen,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paint-Anything 提出一种统一的十六进制颜色提示接口，通过对象级颜色监督实现任意 24 位十六进制颜色（任意颜色）的图像生成与编辑。构建了 Paint-500K 数据管线、纯色锚点辅助监督以及 ACBench 基准，在 FLUX.2-4B 上显著提升颜色保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业设计需要"任意颜色控制"，即能用任意 24 位十六进制值指定目标颜色进行图像生成与编辑。已有工作在颜色生成、编辑和着色上虽有探索，但常依赖专用的颜色表示或专门的推理流程。作者希望借助大语言模型的进展，让即使是紧凑的模型也能将十六进制值与颜色语义关联，从而提供更简单的统一方案，解决任意颜色忠实控制这一挑战。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 学习一个共享的十六进制提示接口用于生成与编辑，通过对象级颜色监督训练；(2) 开发数据管线，从真实图像通过对象定位（object grounding）、感知颜色标注（perceptual color labeling）和编辑配对合成（editing-pair synthesis）构建 Paint-500K 数据集；(3) 由于阴影使真实图像标签只是近似颜色，补充"纯色锚点"（pure-color anchors）监督——这些锚点的像素与配对的十六进制值完全一致，且仅在噪声较高的时间步使用，低噪声训练仍用自然图像。此外提出 ACBench 基准（含 ACBench-T2I 与 ACBench-Edit）来度量两个任务的对象级十六进制颜色保真度。

Result: 在 FLUX.2-4B 上，Paint-Anything 相对基础模型将 ACBench-T2I 和 ACBench-Edit 分数分别提升 85.3% 和 28.3%；消融实验支持该训练方案；在对比方法中取得了最高的平均 CompColor 分数。

Conclusion: Paint-Anything 通过共享的十六进制提示接口和对象级颜色监督，配合纯色锚点的高噪声步训练策略，有效实现了任意颜色的图像生成与编辑，并在颜色保真度基准上超越基础模型及现有对比方法，验证了其训练方案的有效性。

Abstract: Professional design requires any-color control: the ability to specify an object's target color with any 24-bit hex value for image generation and editing. Prior work has explored color generation, editing, and colorization, but often relies on dedicated color representations or specialized inference procedures. Advances in large language models offer a simpler starting point: even compact models can associate hex values with color semantics. We present Paint-Anything, which learns a shared hex-prompt interface for generation and editing through object-level color supervision. We develop a data pipeline that constructs Paint-500K from real images through object grounding, perceptual color labeling, and editing-pair synthesis. Since shadows make real-image labels only approximate colors, we complement this supervision with pure-color anchors whose pixels exactly match their paired hex values. These anchors are used only at high-noise timesteps, leaving low-noise training to natural images. We further introduce Any Color Benchmark (ACBench), comprising ACBench-T2I and ACBench-Edit, to measure object-level hex color fidelity across both tasks. On FLUX.2-4B, Paint-Anything improves ACBench-T2I and ACBench-Edit scores by 85.3% and 28.3%, respectively, relative to the base model, with ablations supporting the training recipe. It also achieves the highest average CompColor score among the compared methods.

</details>


### [99] [FAMOS: Feed-Forward 3D Articulation Modeling from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20817)
*Kevin Qu,Tao Sun,Massimiliano Viola,Liyuan Zhu,Zhizhuo Zhou,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Konrad Schindler,Iro Armeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAMOS是一种前馈模型，能从稀疏、无序的部分点云集合中预测可移动部件分割和关节参数。它通过Multi-state Articulation Transformer在多个观测间联合推理，引入观测关节范围目标（articulation span objective），并利用程序化数据生成器在训练中合成带自标注的资产，在多个数据集上优于前馈和基于优化的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏单目视角建模铰接物体具有挑战性，因为每次观测只揭示部分几何和运动证据。大多数前馈方法从单一观测推断铰接关系，严重依赖学习的类别级形状先验，难以应对部分遮挡和稀疏观测场景。

Method: FAMOS从稀疏无序的部分点云集合中联合推理可移动部件分割和关节参数。核心架构为Multi-state Articulation Transformer，采用交替的state-wise（状态内）和global（全局）注意力来跨观测聚合铰接线索；支持可变输入数量（包括单视图）。提出观测关节范围目标（observed articulation span objective）作为监督，约束每个部件在输入观测中表现出的运动范围，促使模型充分利用完整观测集。此外，设计了程序化数据生成器（procedural data generator），在训练期间合成自标注资产以扩展数据集规模和多样性。

Result: 在PartNet-Mobility、ACD和ArtiCraft-10K数据集上的实验表明，FAMOS在可移动部件分割和关节参数预测上均一致优于前馈和基于优化的基线方法。

Conclusion: FAMOS验证了从稀疏多观测联合推理而非依赖单一观测和类别先验的有效性，通过跨观测注意力聚合、运动范围监督和数据生成策略，在铰接物体建模任务上取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Modeling articulated objects from sparse monocular views is challenging because each observation reveals only partial geometry and motion evidence. Most feed-forward methods infer articulation from a single observation and therefore rely heavily on learned category-level shape priors. We present FAMOS, a feed-forward model that predicts movable-part segmentation and joint parameters from a sparse, unordered set of partial point clouds. Our model jointly reasons over multiple observations and naturally supports a variable number of inputs, including a single view. To aggregate articulation cues across observations, we introduce a Multi-state Articulation Transformer with alternating state-wise and global attention. We further propose an observed articulation span objective that supervises the motion range each part exhibits across the input observations, encouraging the model to leverage the full observation set. To overcome the limited scale and diversity of existing datasets, we introduce a procedural data generator that synthesizes self-annotated assets during training. Experiments on PartNet-Mobility, ACD, and ArtiCraft-10K demonstrate consistent improvements over both feed-forward and optimization-based baselines. Project page: https://kevinqu7.github.io/famos

</details>


### [100] [SplashSplat: Reconstructing Splashing Liquids from Real-World Multi-View Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20818)
*Peiyu Liu,Dingxi Zhang,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Christina Tsalicoglou,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次引入同步多视角飞溅液体重建基准（20个真实场景，7台4K相机@60fps），并提出SplashSplat方法：仅在观测可约束处施加物理结构——由掩码融合的逐帧SDF提供几何，水平集传输得到粗速度场，沿流平流的拉格朗日载体逐观测修正并在丢失处重新播种，解码局部高斯可微渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞溅液体寿命极短（薄片碎裂成韧带和液滴）、外观依赖视点且近乎无纹理、难以长期追踪，导致重建研究集中于烟雾、合成液体或缓慢形变表面。目前尚不存在同步多视角的飞溅液体数据集。

Method: 1) 构建含20个真实场景（从连续水流到剧烈飞溅）的基准，由7台同步、标定的4K相机@60fps采集，配有手工精修的逐视角液体和容器掩码及固定评估划分。2) 提出SplashSplat，遵循"仅在观测可约束处施加物理结构"原则：逐帧液体SDF（由掩码融合）提供几何；相邻SDF间的水平集传输获得粗速度场；沿此流平流的拉格朗日载体，通过每次新观测校正并在覆盖丢失处重新播种，解码局部高斯用于可微渲染。

Result: SplashSplat在真实采集和合成基准上均优于当前最先进的动态高斯溅射方法，具有物理上更合理的运动及更低的训练成本；同一表示无需重新优化即可支持时间插值和风格迁移。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了飞溅液体多视角数据集空白，并证明"仅在观测可约束处施加物理结构"的混合表示（SDF几何+水平集速度场+拉格朗日高斯载体）能在高频、无纹理、短寿命液体上实现高效且物理合理的重建，且具备通用表示的可扩展性（插值和风格迁移）。

Abstract: A splash lives for a fraction of a second: sheets tear into ligaments and droplets, appearance is view-dependent and nearly textureless, and little persists long enough to track. Reconstruction research has consequently focused on smoke, synthetic liquids, or gently deforming surfaces. To our knowledge, no synchronized multi-view dataset of splashing liquids exists. We therefore introduce a benchmark of 20 real scenes, from coherent streams to violent splashes, captured by seven synchronized, calibrated 4K cameras at 60 fps, with manually refined per-view liquid and container masks and fixed evaluation splits. We further present SplashSplat, built on a single principle: impose physical structure only where the observations can constrain it. Per-frame liquid SDFs fused from the masks provide the geometry, level-set transport between consecutive SDFs yields a coarse velocity field, and Lagrangian carriers advected along this flow, corrected against each new observation and reseeded where coverage is lost, decode local Gaussians for differentiable rendering. SplashSplat outperforms state-of-the-art dynamic Gaussian splatting methods on our real captures and on a synthetic benchmark, with physically more plausible motion and a lower training cost. The same representation supports temporal interpolation and style transfer without re-optimization.

</details>


### [101] [Can 4D Foundation Models Remember?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20819)
*Guangzhao He,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Wei-Chiu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PersistBench，一个基于360°视频作为全知真值的基准数据集与指标套件，用于以物体为中心的方式评估4D基础模型（如相机可控视频模型和4D重建模型）的视觉记忆能力，从物体恒存性、运动连续性和外观保持三个维度进行评测。实验发现现有模型只能维持短期一致性，一旦物体离开视野，记忆能力便显著下降，揭示了"看见不等于记住"的能力鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的4D基础模型能够感知和重建动态环境，但它们对所感知内容的"记忆"能力尚未得到有效评估。现有基准依赖像素级指标，且当物体离开视野后缺乏真值，无法以物体为中心的方式参照参考进行视觉记忆评测，这是该工作要填补的空白。

Method: PersistBench利用360°视频作为全知真值（omniscient ground truth），构建数据集和指标套件。提出三个评测维度：物体恒存性（object permanence）、运动连续性（motion continuity）和外观保持（appearance preservation），并在多样类别上评估各类模型。

Result: 对多类模型的评测结果显示，当前模型只能维持短期的一致性，当物体离开视野后其一致性显著下降，说明现有4D模型缺乏稳健的视觉记忆能力。

Conclusion: 该研究清晰揭示了当前模型能力与稳健视觉记忆之间的差距，即"seeing is not remembering"（看见不等于记住），为未来4D基础模型的发展提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: Perceiving and remembering the visual world is fundamental to navigating and interacting with our environment. Current 4D foundation models, such as camera-controllable video models or 4D reconstruction models, can perceive and reconstruct dynamic environments, but how well they remember what they have perceived remains an open question. Existing benchmarks largely rely on pixel-level metrics and lack ground truth for objects once they leave the field of view, making them unable to evaluate visual memory in an object-centric manner against references. To fill this gap, we introduce PersistBench, a dataset and metric suite that leverages 360° videos as omniscient ground truth and proposes three evaluation aspects: object permanence, motion continuity, and appearance preservation. Evaluating various models across diverse categories reveals that current models can only maintain short-term consistency that degrades significantly once objects leave the field of view. Our findings highlight the gap between current model capabilities and robust visual memory ("seeing is not remembering"), providing guidance for future development of 4D foundation models. Dataset and code are available on the project page: https://guangzhaohe.com/persistbench.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Modality Discrepancy Transformer for Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19148)
*Shiyu Luo,Yu Wang,Jiawen Huang,Zhaoxiang Xiao,Chenxi Huang,Qi Zhang,Bin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出模态差异Transformer（MDT），用于在临床视频中自动识别矛盾与犹豫（A/H）情感状态。MDT通过将原始6-token设计扩展为9-token表示（含三个模态嵌入、三个绝对差值特征和三个Hadamard乘积差异特征），结合Transformer自注意力、FiLM文本条件调制和LoRA微调，并加入文本引导的晚期融合分支，在3rd ABAW挑战赛的BAH数据集上实现了0.7408的Macro F1（标注测试集）和0.7368（私有排行榜），比最强公开基线高出10余点，且单GPU训练时间不足20分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矛盾与犹豫（A/H）是跨面部、声音和语言通道表达矛盾信号的情感状态。自动识别A/H的核心难点在于检测跨模态不一致性——而这正是标准多模态融合方法通常会抑制的信号。因此需要一种专门设计以保留和利用跨模态差异信息的融合框架。

Method: MDT基于Bekhouche等人的冲突感知多模态融合框架，将原始6-token设计扩展为9-token表示：三个模态嵌入、三个绝对差值特征和三个Hadamard乘积差异特征（均通过线性投影学习）。九个token送入Transformer自注意力，并以FiLM-based文本条件调制和LoRA微调作为核心架构组件。推理阶段采用文本引导的晚期融合分支，将纯文本辅助头与完整多模态输出融合。

Result: 在3rd ABAW挑战赛的BAH数据集上，MDT在标注测试集上达到0.7408 Macro F1，在私有排行榜上达到0.7368，比最强公开基线高出10余个点；同时训练效率高，单GPU训练时间不足20分钟。

Conclusion: MDT通过显式建模跨模态差异（绝对差值和Hadamard乘积）而非抑制它们，有效解决了标准融合方法在A/H识别中的缺陷，在性能上大幅超越现有基线，同时保持极高的训练效率，为临床场景中的情感状态识别提供了高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are affective states in which individuals express contradictory signals across facial, vocal, and linguistic channels. Automatically recognising A/H in clinical videos requires detecting cross-modal disagreement -- the signal that standard fusion methods suppress. Based on the conflict-aware multimodal fusion framework of Bekhouche et al., we present the Modality Discrepancy Transformer (MDT). MDT enriches the original 6-token design to a 9-token representation comprising three modality embeddings, three absolute-difference features, and three Hadamard-product discrepancy features learned through linear projections. These nine tokens undergo Transformer self-attention, with FiLM-based text-conditioned modulation and LoRA fine-tuning as core architectural components. A text-guided late fusion branch blends a text-only auxiliary head with the full multimodal output at inference. On the BAH dataset from the 3rd ABAW Challenge, MDT achieves 0.7408 Macro F1 on the labelled test split and 0.7368 on the private leaderboard, outperforming the strongest published baseline by over 10 points while training in under 20 minutes on a single GPU.

</details>


### [103] [Subliminal Prompting Beyond Static Geometry: Causal Depth and Multi-Token Confounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19149)
*Barath Velmurugan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对"潜意识学习"（subliminal learning）现象，系统拆解并分别测量了语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B/70B与Qwen）如何在"动物-数字"提示协议中传递隐藏特征。研究发现：固定输出向量相似性预测行为的能力随模型增大而下降（均值变化-0.080）；固定输出头读出无显著变化；而"复制-控制"实验显示捐赠者控制AUC从0.254升至0.540（配对变化+0.286），证明存在因果性时序控制。多token测量（Qwen逐位打分）不恢复正向单token关联，逐token平均产生的正向关联在控制数字宽度后消失，暴露出长度混淆。结论：固定几何、可观测可读性、因果时序与多token测量是冻结提示通道的不同属性，它们约束了token级解释，但未确定训练期特征迁移的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于"token纠缠"（token entanglement）解释潜意识学习的测量各自回答不同的问题（输出是否共变、固定输出向量是否对齐、隐藏状态能否读出答案、该状态是否因果控制答案），缺乏对每种属性进行独立、隔离的测量。本文旨在分别量化这些属性，以约束token级解释并厘清混淆因素（如长度混淆）。

Method: 在固定的"动物-数字"提示协议下进行四种独立测量：(1) 固定输出向量相似性（预测行为）；(2) 固定输出头读出（归一化深度AUC）；(3) 因果控制测试——将数字提示中临时答案位置的状态复制到另一数字提示的五个深度，测量最终动物分数跟随哪个提示，得到捐赠者控制AUC；(4) 多token测量（在Qwen模型中对每个数字逐位打分）。并设置特异性与身份对照，控制数字宽度以排除长度混淆。

Result: Llama-3.1-8B到70B：固定输出向量相似性预测行为变差，配对相关均值变化-0.080（95%CI [-0.127,-0.035]）；固定输出头读出在归一化深度AUC上无明确变化。因果控制测试中，捐赠者控制AUC从0.254升至0.540，配对变化+0.286（95%CI [+0.272,+0.300]），全部18个概念均上升；在剩余正好8个Transformer块时对比仍然保持，特异性与身份对照保持很小或精确。Qwen模型中逐位打分不恢复正向单token关联，逐token平均造成正向合并关联，但在控制数字宽度后消失，揭示出长度混淆。

Conclusion: 固定几何、可观测可读性、因果时序性以及多token测量是这一冻结提示通道的不同属性，它们彼此区分。这些结果约束了token级（纠缠）解释，但并未确定训练期特征迁移（trait transfer）的具体机制。

Abstract: Subliminal learning shows that language models can transmit a hidden trait through outputs that appear unrelated to it. One proposed explanation, token entanglement, links animal and number tokens through the model's output vocabulary. Yet existing measurements answer different questions: whether outputs co-vary, fixed output vectors align, an answer can be read from a hidden state, or that state causally controls the answer. We measure each separately in a fixed animal-number prompting protocol. From Llama-3.1-8B to 70B, fixed output-vector similarity predicts behavior less well: the paired mean correlation change is -0.080 (95% CI [-0.127, -0.035]). A fixed output-head readout shows no resolved change in normalized depth AUC. To test control, we copy the temporary answer-position state from one number prompt into another at five depths and measure which prompt the final animal score follows. Donor-control AUC rises from 0.254 to 0.540, a paired change of +0.286 (95% CI [+0.272, +0.300]), with increases for all 18 concepts. The contrast remains with exactly eight transformer blocks remaining, while specificity and identity controls remain small or exact. In two Qwen models, scoring every digit in sequence does not recover the positive one-token association. Per-token averaging instead creates a positive pooled association that disappears after controlling number width, revealing a length confound. Thus, fixed geometry, observational readability, causal timing, and multi-token measurement are distinct properties of this frozen prompting channel. They constrain token-level explanations but do not identify the mechanism of training-time trait transfer.

</details>


### [104] [Sampling Reveals Style: Unsupervised, Training-Free Discovery of Prompt-Conditional Stylistic Axes in LLM Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19150)
*Ajit Mallavarapu,Ziwei Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的、提示条件化的方法来发现大语言模型（LLM）中显著文体维度：通过在高温下对单一提示进行重复采样，对汇集后的隐藏激活进行主成分分析（PCA），并从极坐标生成中自动标注所得轴。通过与245个人类诱导的文体标注进行两阶段验证，在最强模型（Qwen-3.5-4B-Instruct）上，前两个轴匹配人类自发请求维度达到72.8%精度和43.6%宏召回，75.6%有效性评分认为极坐标生成准确，相邻注释者间一致性达90.9%。可发现性高度依赖模型，DeepSeek-7B-Chat精度骤降至35.3%（其主导成分被结构而非文体方差支配）。简单的主成分分析可以作为探测LLM表征文体结构的低成本有效方法，同时揭示模型间在结构组织上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的隐藏激活中编码了丰富的文体结构，但通常发现给定提示下哪些文体维度显著需要监督对比数据。作者希望提出一种训练免费的替代方案，无需监督数据即可自动发现并标注提示条件下的显著文体维度，并探究不同模型间这种结构组织的差异。

Method: 方法为训练免费、提示条件化的发现流程：在高温下反复采样单一提示的完成（completions），对汇集后的隐藏激活进行主成分分析（PCA），并从极坐标生成（pole generations）自动标注所得轴。随后在两个阶段的研究中，将发现的轴与245个人类诱导的文体标注对照验证，评估精度、召回率、有效性评分及注释者间一致性。

Result: 在最强模型Qwen-3.5-4B-Instruct上，前两个轴匹配人类自发请求维度达72.8%精度和43.6%宏召回；75.6%有效性评分判定极坐标生成与其标签准确匹配，相邻互注者一致性90.9%。发现高度依赖模型：Qwen系列和Llama-3.2-3B能暴露人类显著轴，而DeepSeek-7B-Chat精度降至35.3%，其主导成分被结构而非文体方差支配。

Conclusion: 简单地对模型自身解码方差进行主成分分析，可以作为一种有效、低成本的手段来探测LLM表征中的文体结构，同时还能揭示不同模型间该结构组织的显著差异，为理解LLM内部文体表征提供了无需训练的实用方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) encode rich stylistic structure in their hidden activations, but discovering which stylistic dimensions are salient for a given prompt typically requires supervised contrastive data. We present a training-free, prompt-conditional alternative: we repeatedly sample completions of a single prompt at elevated temperature, apply Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to the pooled hidden activations, and label the resulting axes automatically from the pole generations. We validate the discovered axes against 245 human-elicited stylistic annotations in a two-phase study. On our strongest model (Qwen-3.5-4B-Instruct), the top two axes match spontaneously requested human dimensions with 72.8% precision and 43.6% macro-recall, and 75.6% of validity ratings judge the axes' polar generations accurate to their labels, with 90.9% adjacent inter-annotator agreement. Discoverability is strongly model-dependent: both Qwen models and Llama-3.2-3B expose human-salient axes, while DeepSeek-7B-Chat drops to 35.3% precision, its leading components dominated by structural rather than stylistic variance. Simple PCA over a model's own decoding variance is thus an effective, low-cost probe of stylistic structure in LLM representations, one that also exposes sharp cross-model differences in how that structure is organized.

</details>


### [105] [What Users Think of Generative AI: A Cross-Platform NLP Analysis of Trust and Friction in App Store Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19151)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Umme Nusrat Jahan,Shouvaggo Sharif Shammo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对六大主流生成式AI应用（ChatGPT、Gemini、Microsoft Copilot、Claude、DeepSeek、Perplexity）的17,012条英文应用商店评论进行了跨应用分析，结合BERTopic主题建模与RoBERTa情感分类，并通过卡方检验、Kruskal-Wallis检验及Bonferroni校正的多项逻辑回归评估跨应用差异。结果显示负面情感集中于广告、认证、服务器可靠性和订阅定价；各应用间情感存在显著差异，Claude呈现最高的负面情感（47.7%）与强烈热衷用户并存，构成统计显著的极化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI应用虽已实现快速消费者普及，但缺乏大规模研究来考察用户感知质量、信任度与采纳障碍。现有研究多为单一应用或小样本分析，缺少跨应用的大规模比较，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 收集Google Play与Apple App Store上六大GenAI应用的17,012条英文评论；采用BERTopic进行主题建模，结合RoBERTa进行情感分类；运用卡方检验、Kruskal-Wallis检验及Bonferroni校正的多项逻辑回归评估跨应用差异；使用300条评论的分层样本对两种方法进行人工编码验证；并提出Trust Friction Score作为信任与可用性障碍的综合衡量指标。

Result: 负面情感集中于广告（91%）、认证（89%）、服务器可靠性（83%）和订阅定价（73%）；各应用情感差异显著，Claude负面情感最高（47.7%）且伴随强烈的用户热情，呈现统计显著的极化。发现对评论数量不均具有稳健性。此外，部分DeepSeek评论涉及与其中国来源相关的地缘政治和数据隐私关切（作为探索性观察）。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了经人工验证、可操作的证据，阐明消费者GenAI应用中的用户信任、可用性与采纳障碍，并通过Trust Friction Score将应用特定的信任与可用性障碍归纳为可解释维度，为后续研究和产品改进提供依据。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) applications have achieved rapid consumer adoption, yet little large-scale research examines user-perceived quality, trust, and adoption barriers. We present one of the first cross-application analyses of app store reviews for six major GenAI applications (ChatGPT, Gemini, Microsoft Copilot, Claude, DeepSeek, and Perplexity), comprising 17,012 English-language reviews from Google Play and the Apple App Store. We combine BERTopic topic modeling with RoBERTa sentiment classification and evaluate cross-application differences using chi-square, Kruskal-Wallis, and multinomial logistic regression with Bonferroni correction. Both components are validated against human coding using a stratified sample of 300 reviews. Results show that negative sentiment concentrates in advertising (91%), authentication (89%), server reliability (83%), and subscription pricing (73%). Sentiment differs significantly across applications, with Claude exhibiting the highest negative sentiment (47.7%) alongside a strongly enthusiastic user base, indicating statistically significant polarization. These findings are robust despite unequal review counts across applications. As exploratory observations, a subset of DeepSeek reviews raised geopolitical and data privacy concerns related to its Chinese origin, while a proposed Trust Friction Score summarizes application-specific trust and usability barriers into interpretable dimensions. The study provides validated and actionable evidence on user trust, usability, and adoption barriers in consumer generative AI applications.

</details>


### [106] [FakeSpotter: A content and strategy agnostic Viral Misinformation Detection Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19152)
*Giovanni Spitale,Federico Germani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FakeSpotter，一种内容与策略无关的虚假信息风险检测工具，通过测量文本的结构性指纹（而非直接裁定真伪）来评估病毒性虚假信息风险，在764条文本语料上对短文和长文分别取得0.788和0.793的宏观F1分数，并提供可解释的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测工具依赖二元真/假分类或在历史样本上训练的模型，当出现新型误导性叙事时实用性受限。因此需要一种能够识别虚假信息结构特征、不依赖历史示例且具有可解释性的检测方法。

Method: FakeSpotter基于理论驱动框架，在语言学、叙事、逻辑和批判性思维四个维度测量虚假信息的结构指纹而非直接判定真伪。采用重复LLM评估，并针对短文和长文分别训练领域特定的逻辑回归分类器。其解释层通过基于特征的评分、信号一致性和警示指数提供可解释输出，可用于社会聆听。

Result: 在来自社交媒体和FakeNewsNet的764条标注文本语料上，FakeSpotter在保留测试集上对短文取得0.788的宏观F1分数，对长文取得0.793的宏观F1分数。

Conclusion: 识别虚假信息的结构性指纹能够支持对潜在病毒性虚假信息进行早期、可解释且人类监督下的评估。

Abstract: Misinformation detection tools often rely on binary true and false classifications or models trained on historical examples, limiting their usefulness when novel misleading narratives emerge. Here, we present FakeSpotter, a content- and strategy-agnostic tool designed to estimate the viral misinformation risk of textual content by measuring structural fingerprints of misinformation rather than directly adjudicating truthfulness. FakeSpotter operationalizes a theory-driven framework across linguistic, narrative, logical, and critical-thinking dimensions, using repeated LLM assessments and domain-specific logistic regression classifiers for short and long texts. In a labelled corpus of 764 texts from social media and FakeNewsNet, FakeSpotter achieved macro F1 scores of 0.788 for short texts and 0.793 for long texts on a held-out test set. FakeSpotter's interpretive layer provides explainable outputs through feature-based scores, signal agreement, and a caution index, and can be used for social listening. These findings suggest that identifying the structural fingerprints of misinformation can support early, explainable, and human-supervised assessment of potentially viral misinformation.

</details>


### [107] [Stop Removing Stopwords: How an Inherited Preprocessing Default Distorts Legal Text-as-Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19153)
*Gregory M. Dickinson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对经验法学研究中文本即数据（text-as-data）管道里最根深蒂固的预处理默认步骤——停用词去除——进行了系统性的单字消融实验，用最高法院数据库标签匹配Caselaw Access Project判决文本，在两个F1余量差异显著的二分类任务上（意识形态方向约0.68，宪法/非宪法法律类型约0.92）逐一剔除约18,500个候选词并直接测量其对下游目标的效应。三个发现：通用停用词表在所有测试中均低于不去除任何词的基线；即使优化后的停用词表与不去除在统计上也无显著差异；基于词级特征的元模型无法预测哪些去除有帮助。结论是保留停用词关乎测量效度，该方法可推广到任何继承的预处理默认步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验法学研究中大量文本即数据的管道仍依赖稀疏、可解释的管线（TF-IDF特征与线性分类器），因为文本特征本身常是研究对象而非仅预测手段。但这些管线继承了上世纪中期信息检索时代的预处理默认设置，从未针对分类准确率进行验证，其中最难撼动的是停用词去除。作者希望直接以下游目标来评估这一默认步骤，检验其是否损害了研究所要恢复的教义与意识形态信号。

Method: 引入一种穷尽式单字消融方法：对停用词去除这一最顽固的预处理步骤，逐一剔除约18,500个候选词并直接测量其对下游分类目标的效应，以逼近任何专家所能构建的最佳停用词表。将最高法院数据库标签与Caselaw Access Project判决文本匹配，在两个F1余量呈对照的任务（意识形态方向，基线F1约0.68；宪法/非宪法法律类型，约0.92）上分别考察了7,668和7,001份判决。同时训练基于词级特征的元模型，检验能否预测哪些词的去除有益。

Result: 三个主要发现：其一，通用停用词表在每一项测试中均低于不进行去除的基线；其二，即便经过优化的停用词表，其效果与什么都不去除在统计上无法区分；其三，基于词级特征训练的元模型无法预测哪些词的去除能够带来帮助，因此词表筛选缺乏可瞄准的目标。

Conclusion: 停用词去除作为继承性的预处理默认步骤，会悄然重塑模型所见特征，可能扭曲经验法学文本即数据研究所要恢复的教义与意识形态信号。因此应把保留停用词视为测量效度问题。该单字消融方法可推广至任何继承的预处理默认设置，对其他可解释文本分析管道具有警示意义。

Abstract: Empirical legal scholarship increasingly treats judicial text as data, and much of it still runs on sparse, interpretable pipelines -- TF-IDF features and linear classifiers -- because the textual feature is often the object of study, not merely a means to a prediction. Yet these pipelines inherit a chain of preprocessing defaults from mid-century information retrieval that were never validated against classification accuracy, the most entrenched being stopword removal. This study introduces an exhaustive single-word ablation that measures a preprocessing step's effect directly against the downstream objective, and applies it to stopword removal as the hardest case to dislodge. Matching Supreme Court Database labels to Caselaw Access Project opinion texts, it examines two binary tasks that bracket F1 headroom, ideological direction (no-removal baseline F1 ~ 0.68) and constitutional versus non-constitutional law type (~ 0.92), across 7,668 and 7,001 opinions. For each task the analysis approximates the best stoplist any expert could build, removing each of roughly 18,500 candidate words and measuring the effect directly. Three findings follow: generic stoplists in common use fall below the no-removal baseline in every test; even optimized stoplists are statistically indistinguishable from removing nothing; and meta-models trained on word-level features cannot predict which removals help, so list curation has nothing to target. The method generalizes to any inherited preprocessing default, and the result is a caution specific to interpretable legal text-as-data: a step that silently reshapes which features a model sees can distort the very doctrinal and ideological signal such research exists to recover. Leaving stopwords in place is a question of measurement validity.

</details>


### [108] [Neo-Classic: A Benchmark for Evaluating Linguistic-Aesthetic Reasoning in Classical Chinese Poetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19154)
*Han Zhang,Zihan Gu,Zhiyuan Wang,Tianyi Ma,Jiacheng Lu,Xinyan Zhang,Yuhao Wei,Cheng Hua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文介绍Neo-Classic基准，用于评估LLM在古典诗歌上的语言-审美推理能力，通过当代专家创作的严格格律诗（样本外）和反向理解探针，发现模型在从历史文本迁移到当代文本时准确率下降20-50%，在篇章级排序任务上准确率仅0-13%，即使有专家指导也仅达36%，表明模型擅长局部形式模式但缺乏全局层次规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有古典诗歌基准上LLM准确率虽高，但难以区分真正的语言-审美推理与对预训练模式的依赖；需要新的基准来评估模型的迁移能力和深层推理。

Method: 构建Neo-Classic基准，包含由当代专家创作的严格格律诗（样本外）及一套反向理解探针；对Qwen3-Max、Gemini-3-Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2等模型进行五项行为探针测试，考察层次化约束满足能力。

Result: 历史文本到当代文本的准确率下降20-50%；篇章级排序任务标准准确率仅0-13%；加入专家级指导后，推理增强模型提升至36%，但仍与人类专家有显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前LLM虽能捕捉局部形式模式，但在需要全局层次规划的语言-审美推理上仍显不足，说明其推理能力存在本质局限。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve high accuracy on established Classical Chinese Poetry benchmarks, it remains challenging to distinguish transferable Linguistic-Aesthetic Reasoning from reliance on familiar pre-training patterns. To address this issue, we introduce Neo-Classic, an evaluation benchmark that combines a constructionist Out-of-Sample (OOS) dataset with a suite of reverse understanding probes. Unlike traditional benchmarks that rely on verification or generation over historical corpora, Neo-Classic comprises strictly metrical poetry authored by contemporary experts, reducing the possibility of direct retrieval. We evaluate state-of-the-art models, including Qwen3-Max, Gemini-3-Pro, and DeepSeek-V3.2, across five behavioral probes designed to test hierarchical constraint satisfaction. Our results reveal two primary limitations. First, a performance gap of 20 to 50 percent emerges when models transition from historical to contemporary texts. Second, models exhibit substantial difficulties in discourse-level ordering tasks, with standard accuracy remaining low (0 to 13 percent). Although expert-level guidance improves the performance of reasoning-enhanced models to 36 percent, a notable gap with human experts persists. These findings suggest that while current LLMs capture local formal patterns, they struggle with global hierarchical planning required for robust Linguistic-Aesthetic Reasoning.

</details>


### [109] [Towards Proactive Detection of User-Side Implicit Conflicts in Human-LLM Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19155)
*Jinqiang Wang,Tao Zhu,Huansheng Ning*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于人机对话中用户侧隐含冲突的检测问题。作者构建了人类标注基准UC-Bench以评估用户侧冲突检测，发现现有LLM在此任务上表现不佳。为提升轻量级LLM，作者提出SynUC方法：在约束空间中表示用户侧冲突，并利用SPEAKING框架指导可追踪的约束变换，进而从WildChat构建了含2,487条样本的训练集UC-Data。实验表明，基于UC-Data训练的Qwen3.5-4B在UC-Bench上超越了Claude Opus 4.8等更大规模的通用LLM，也优于用现有方法合成数据训练的同主干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类与LLM的对话中，用户的后续话语可能隐式地与早期意图冲突，导致LLM误解用户需求并生成不恰当回复。可靠的对话系统应在生成回复前主动检测此类用户侧冲突并在必要时寻求澄清。然而，先前工作多聚焦于LLM侧冲突，用户侧冲突尚未被充分探索。此外，现有数据合成方法未显式建模历史与当前用户话语间的隐含不兼容性，难以捕捉冲突演化并生成可靠标注的隐含冲突样本。

Method: 论文提出两大贡献：(1) 构建人类标注基准UC-Bench用于评估用户侧冲突检测任务；(2) 提出SynUC，一种约束引导的合成方法：在约束空间中表示用户侧冲突，利用SPEAKING框架指导可追踪的约束变换。该方法应用于WildChat构建了用户侧冲突训练集UC-Data（含2,487条样本），用于训练轻量级LLM。

Result: 初步实验表明现有LLM在用户侧冲突检测任务上表现欠佳，尤其当冲突源于对话历史中隐含不兼容性时更为困难。在UC-Bench上，基于UC-Data训练的Qwen3.5-4B表现优于Claude Opus 4.8等更大规模的通用LLM，也优于使用现有方法合成数据训练的同主干模型。

Conclusion: 用户侧冲突检测是对话系统中尚未充分探索但至关重要的任务。SynUC通过显式建模历史与当前话语间的隐含不兼容性（约束空间表示+可追踪约束变换）有效提升了合成数据的质量，使轻量级LLM仅凭有限训练数据即可在用户侧冲突检测上超越大规模通用模型，验证了该方法的有效性与数据合成的价值。

Abstract: In Human-LLM dialogue, follow-up user utterances may implicitly conflict with earlier intents, leading the LLM to misinterpret user needs and generate inappropriate responses. A reliable dialogue system should proactively detect user-side conflicts before generating a response and seek clarification when necessary. However, prior work has largely focused on LLM-side conflicts, leaving user-side conflicts underexplored. To fill this gap, we construct UC-Bench, a human-annotated benchmark for evaluating user-side conflict detection. Preliminary experiments show that existing LLMs struggle with this task, especially when conflicts arise from implicit incompatibilities grounded in dialogue history. To improve lightweight LLMs with limited training data, we investigate data synthesis for user-side conflict detection. Existing synthesis methods do not explicitly model the implicit incompatibilities between historical and current user utterances, making it difficult to capture the evolution of conflicts and to generate reliably labeled implicit conflict samples. We propose SynUC, a constraint-guided synthesis method that represents user-side conflicts in a constraint space and uses the SPEAKING framework to guide traceable constraint transformations. Applying SynUC to WildChat, we construct UC-Data, a user-side conflict training set containing 2,487 samples. On UC-Bench, Qwen3.5-4B trained on UC-Data outperforms larger general-purpose LLMs such as Claude Opus 4.8, as well as the same backbone trained on data synthesized by existing methods.

</details>


### [110] [Reflective Recovery: A Self-Supervised Method for Reasoning by Learning from Mistakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19156)
*Qirui Chen,Renjie Pi,Jiahui Gao,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflective Recovery，一种简单有效的自监督方法，将失败的推理尝试转化为恢复训练数据。通过提取失败轨迹的初始片段并与提示连接，引导LLM走向有效解决方案，从而教会模型在推理过程中识别和纠正错误。该方法在AIME 2025上将DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B的准确率从30.0%提升至37.5%，在Minerva上从37.6%提升至47.8%，并打破了缩放塌陷（Scaling Collapse）障碍，使模型发展出突现的自我纠正行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流基于完美推理轨迹的模仿学习方法在问题集有限时会出现缩放塌陷：增加正例无法带来持续改进。同时，LLM在推理时无法保证每个中间步骤都正确，容易出错且难以从错误中恢复，甚至被先前错误的累积进一步误导。

Method: 一种自监督方法Reflective Recovery：提取失败轨迹的初始片段，与提示词拼接，用于引导LLM走向有效解决方案。由于这些失败片段很可能包含错误，该过程教会模型在推理中识别并纠正错误，无需外部评论者或奖励模型即可从错误状态恢复。

Result: 在多个基准上的评估表明，Reflective Recovery显著提升性能：在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上，AIME 2025准确率从30.0%提升至37.5%，Minerva准确率从37.6%提升至47.8%。分析表明它打破了缩放塌陷障碍，使模型发展出突现的自我纠正行为。

Conclusion: 该方法代表了从面向结果的记忆（outcome-oriented memorization）到面向过程的反思推理（process-oriented reflective reasoning）的范式转变，无需外部评论器或奖励模型即可实现错误状态的恢复和自我纠正。

Abstract: Data-driven fine-tuning is widely adopted to enhance reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) due to its simplicity and efficiency. However, mainstream imitation learning methods that rely exclusively on perfect reasoning trajectories suffer from a Scaling Collapse: when the problem set is limited, increasing positive examples fails to yield continuous improvement. However, during inference, an LLM can not guarantee that every intermediate step is correct and is therefore prone to errors. Once such errors arise, the LLM often struggles to recover and may be further misled by the accumulation of previous mistakes. To address this, we propose Reflective Recovery, a simple yet effective self-supervised approach that transforms failed reasoning attempts into recovery training data. Specifically, we extract initial segments of failed trajectories, concatenate them with prompts, and use them to guide the LLM toward valid solutions. Because these segments from failed trajectories are likely to contain errors, this process teaches models to recognize and correct mistakes during reasoning, enabling recovery from erroneous states without relying on external critics or reward models. Evaluated on extensive benchmarks, Reflective Recovery significantly improves performance. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, it boosts accuracy from 30.0% to 37.5% on AIME 2025 and from 37.6% to 47.8% on Minerva. More importantly, analyses demonstrate that it breaks the scaling collapse barrier and enables models to develop emergent self-correction behaviors, representing a paradigm shift from outcome-oriented memorization to process-oriented reflective reasoning.

</details>


### [111] [VisKG-LM: Compiling Knowledge Graphs into Visual Memory for Multiple-Choice Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19158)
*Yixin Peng,Er Jin,Shiwei Luo,Diego Collarana,Stefan Decker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VisKG-LM 提出将知识图谱子图离线编译为"关系标注路径"并以图像形式缓存为只读视觉记忆，在推理时仅将问题与候选答案以文本进行语言建模，最后层才读取缓存的视觉记忆。该方法在知识图谱问答中解耦了图编码与语言推理，避免重复在线编码子图，并在 CommonsenseQA、OpenBookQA、MedQA-USMLE 上超越 GreaseLM 与 7B 规模的 GraphVis，且仅用约 400M 在线参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问答方法在每次评分样本时都需从头对检索子图进行在线编码（跨训练轮次、种子和评估运行重复编码），即便知识图谱本身从未改变，造成大量冗余计算。作者质疑能否将检索到的知识图谱一次离线编译，再以只读记忆方式访问，从而将图编码与语言推理解耦。

Method: VisKG-LM 将每个候选答案对应的检索子图序列化为"关系标注路径"（Relation-Labeled Paths），并将其渲染成二维布局保留路径分支结构的图像。每张图像离线编码一次并缓存复用。推理时，语言模型仅从文本理解问题和候选答案，仅在最后一层查询缓存的视觉记忆，同时读取全局布局与局部关系细节——图信息只在文本被理解之后才进入。

Result: 在 CommonsenseQA、OpenBookQA、MedQA-USMLE 测试集上，VisKG-LM 分别比 GreaseLM 提升 1.2、0.8、4.3 分，并匹配或超越 7B 参数量视觉语言模型 GraphVis，而在线参数仅约 400M。相比接收完全相同关系标注路径的纯文本对照，三个基准分别提升 4.2、6.5、5.1 分。

Conclusion: 完整视觉记忆接口相对单纯路径文本化具有额外增益，证明编译式视觉记忆可作为在线图传播（在线编码）之外的可行替代方案，兼顾性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs are usually integrated into question answering by encoding a retrieved subgraph with a graph neural network and fusing it with the language model in the online inference path. The same subgraph is therefore re-encoded from scratch every time a pair is scored, across training epochs, seeds, and evaluation runs, even though the knowledge graph never changes. We ask whether the retrieved knowledge graphs can instead be compiled once, offline, and then accessed as read-only memory. VisKG-LM shows that it can, by decoupling graph encoding from language reasoning. It serializes each retrieved candidate-specific subgraph as Relation-Labeled Paths and renders the result as an image whose two-dimensional layout preserves the branching structure of the paths. Each image is encoded once, offline, and cached for reuse. At inference, the language model contextualizes the question and candidate from text alone, and only its final layer consults the cached visual memory, reading both its global layout and its local relational detail. The graph information thus enters only after the text has been understood. On the test sets of CommonsenseQA, OpenBookQA, and MedQA-USMLE, VisKG-LMimproves over GreaseLM by $1.2$, $0.8$, and $4.3$ points, respectively, while matching or surpassing GraphVis, a $7$B vision-language model, with only about $400$M online parameters. Against a matched text-only control that receives the identical Relation-Labeled Paths, it gains $4.2$, $6.5$, and $5.1$ points across the three benchmarks. These gains show that the complete visual-memory interface adds value beyond path textualization alone and support compiled visual memory as an alternative to online graph propagation.

</details>


### [112] [Advantage Scale Calibration Imbalance in Group-Relative Optimization under Low-Variance Rewards: Diagnosis and Bounded Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19164)
*Fei Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种优势尺度三路校准接口，统一解释并解决 verifier-style RLVR 中组内相对优化的方差问题；基于该接口引入 Reward-Resolution Protocol（过滤亚分辨率抖动）与 MaxNorm-AC（有界基数恢复），在稠密/MoE 架构及数学/代码推理上超越稳健尺度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 verifier-style RLVR 中，组内相对优化常将优势尺度视为实现细节，导致两类低方差情形混淆：亚分辨率抖动（不应成为偏好信号）与可信但小的基数差距（应在不扭曲 KL 校准的前提下被学习）。现有方法如 RLOO/Dr.GRPO 会让可信小差距被 KL 主导，而 GRPO 的标准差分母可能无界放大微小差距。

Method: 提出同一组内尺度分母同时决定奖励分支强度、提示级批次权重以及在原始基数尺度上重表达奖励分支时所诱导的有效 KL 校准，形成三路校准接口。基于此接口，引入 Reward-Resolution Protocol 过滤亚分辨率差距，以及 MaxNorm-AC 为可信非零差距提供有界基数恢复。

Result: 在稠密/MoE 架构以及数学/代码推理任务上，MaxNorm-AC 相对于最强的稳健尺度基线取得改进，同时截断了低方差反比尺度尾部。

Conclusion: 优势尺度并非实现细节，而是影响奖励信号与 KL 校准的关键维度；所提三路接口与两种协议能有效分离低方差噪声与可信信号，提升 RLVR 训练稳定性与性能。

Abstract: In verifier-style RLVR, group-relative optimization often treats advantage scale as an implementation detail. This paper separates two low-variance cases: sub-resolution jitter that should not become a preference signal, and credible but small cardinal gaps that should be learned without distorting KL calibration. We propose an advantage-scale three-way calibration interface: the same within-group scale denominator simultaneously determines the reward-branch strength, prompt-level batch weight, and the effective KL calibration induced when the reward branch is re-expressed on the original cardinal scale. This interface explains why RLOO / Dr.GRPO can let credible small gaps become KL dominated, whereas GRPO's standard-deviation denominator can amplify tiny gaps without bound. Based on this interface, we further introduce the Reward-Resolution Protocol and MaxNorm-AC, respectively filtering sub-resolution gaps and providing bounded cardinal recovery on credible nonzero gaps. Across dense / MoE architectures and math / code reasoning, MaxNorm-AC improves over the strongest robust-scale baseline while truncating the low-variance inverse-scale tail.

</details>


### [113] [To Memories and Beyond: From Remembering to Knowing You across Long-Term Multimodal Personal Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19167)
*Wenqi Zhou,Zhuorui Yu,Kaiao Wen,Hao Zheng,Xinyi Zheng,Peiran Wu,Enmin Zhou,Chi-Hao Wu,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ReaLMem，这是首个基于真实多年期个人视觉档案构建的长期多模态记忆基准，并配套第一人称主观标注。ReaLMem 从事实回忆、人格推断、预测性个性化三个认知层级评估模型。作者还提出 ChronoProfiler 时序加权画像模块，通过计算用户属性的时序稳定性分数作为显著性先验，解决时间上不一致的偏好冲突，并帮助模型复合多个同时活跃的偏好。实验表明预测性个性化是持续瓶颈，MLLM 与记忆系统存在明显差距，高质量的时序感知表征能显著提升个性化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准存在多重缺陷：大多为合成且纯文本、忽略锚定日常人类记忆的视觉记录、缺乏真实且因果相连的纵向数据，因而局限于浅层事实回忆。这成为AI系统作为个性化数字伴侣进行长期个人历史推理（不仅仅是记忆存储，还需追踪纵向经历和演化偏好）这一核心能力的评估瓶颈。

Method: 1) 构建 ReaLMem 基准：基于真实多年期个人视觉档案配第一人称主观标注，从真实生活数据中构建；2) 设计三个认知层级：事实回忆、人格推断、预测性个性化；3) 提出 ChronoProfiler 模块：计算用户属性的时序稳定性分数并作为显著性先验，用于解决时序不一致偏好的冲突、复合多个协同活跃的偏好。

Result: 对前沿多模态大语言模型（MLLM）和记忆系统的大量评估显示：预测性个性化是持续的"天花板"瓶颈；MLLM 与记忆系统之间存在清晰的性能差距和瓶颈；高质量的时序感知表征能显著提升个性化性能。

Conclusion: ReaLMem 提供了真实的测试平台，ChronoProfiler 提供了简单有效的长期个性化机制，二者共同为未来终身AI伴侣的研究奠定基础。

Abstract: As AI systems evolve into personalized digital companions, a central capability is reasoning over a user's long-term personal history: not merely storing past events, but tracking longitudinal experiences and evolving preferences. Progress here is bottlenecked by evaluation, existing long-term memory benchmarks are largely synthetic and text-only, they overlook the visual records that anchor everyday human memory, lack the authentic and causally connected longitudinal data that real personalization demands, and consequently remain confined to shallow factual recall. We introduce ReaLMem (Real-world Long-term Multimodal Memory), the first benchmark built from authentic multi-year personal visual archives, paired with first-person subjective annotations. ReaLMem evaluates models across three cognitive tiers of increasing difficulty: factual recall, persona inference, and predictive personalization. We further propose ChronoProfiler, a temporal-weighting profiling module that computes temporal stability scores for user attributes and applies them as a salience prior, resolving conflicts among temporally inconsistent preferences and helping models compound multiple co-active preferences in complex personalized decisions. Extensive evaluation of frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and memory systems on ReaLMem reveals predictive personalization as a consistent ceiling, exposes clear performance gaps and bottlenecks between MLLMs and memory systems, and shows that high-quality, temporally informed representations substantially improve personalization. Together, ReaLMem and ChronoProfiler provide an authentic testbed and a simple, effective mechanism for long-term personalization, laying a foundation for future research on lifelong AI companions.

</details>


### [114] [YNU-HPCC at SemEval-2025 Task 11: Bridging the Gap in Text-Based Emotion Using Multiple Prediction Headers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19238)
*Hao Yang,Jin Wang,Xuejie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了YNU-HPCC团队在SemEval-2025任务11子任务A（弥合文本情感差距）中的参与情况。最佳系统采用RoBERTa模型（基于Transformer编码器的改进版BERT），并增强了输出头以实现单情感预测。官方排名得分为0.44（涵盖所有语言）。团队使用Google Translate将全部数据集翻译成英文以便后续处理，并通过概率与注意力分析发现：单一预测头优于六头同时预测六种情感，且在统一翻译后的英语数据集上训练效果优于使用原始数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在解决多语言文本情感识别任务中的跨语言差异问题，通过统一翻译策略和模型架构优化来提升情感预测性能，并探索多头情感预测与单头预测的架构设计优劣。

Method: 采用RoBERTa模型作为主干，改造输出头以实现同时处理单一情感；使用Google Translate将全部数据集翻译为英文，实现统一语言处理；通过概率分析与注意力分析验证模型架构选择与数据处理策略的有效性。

Result: 官方排名得分0.44（含所有语言结果）。研究发现：(I)单一预测头比六头同时预测六种情感效果更优；(II)在统一翻译后的英语数据集上训练优于使用原始多语言数据集。

Conclusion: 单情感预测头的架构设计更适合本任务，统一翻译成英语的数据处理策略能有效提升跨语言情感识别性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of the YNU-HPCC team in subtask A of task 11, Bridging the Gap in Text-Based Emotion at SemEval-2025. Our best-performing system employs the RoBERTa (Robustly Optimized BERT Approach) model, an improved version of BERT that utilizes the Transformer encoder architecture. We enhanced the output head to allow the model to process one emotion simultaneously. We obtained the official ranking score (0.44), including results from all languages. The entire dataset was translated into English using Google Translate to facilitate subsequent processing. Through probabilistic and attention analyses, we found that (I) a single prediction head performs better than six heads predicting six emotions simultaneously, and (II) training on a uniformly translated English dataset yields better results than using the original dataset. The code is available at: https://github.com/BGWH123/Semeval-2025-task11.

</details>


### [115] [Why Pretraining Fails to Share Cross-Lingual Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19291)
*Adam Gaber,Uriel Dolev,Elisabeth Fittschen,Bobby Cheng,Yuval Marton,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）跨语言知识泛化能力差的根本原因：词元空间不重叠（disjoint token spaces）。作者通过可控双语预训练实验证明，即使使用完全相同的文本和分词，只要词元空间不重叠，就会导致知识分隔（knowledge compartmentalization）。他们提出通过简单的逐词翻译将语言映射到共享词元空间，可显著提升跨语言知识泛化，恢复高达12.6%的母语学习效率，是基线水平的14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在许多语言的建模上取得了显著进展，但与人类多语者不同，其跨语言知识迁移能力非常有限。这一局限虽已被充分记录，但其在多语训练中的起源仍不清楚。作者旨在探究跨语言知识泛化差的根本原因，并寻找有效干预手段。

Method: 作者预训练了360M和7B参数的LLM，观察跨语言知识泛化能力在预训练过程中的变化。为隔离原因，采用可控双语预训练设置：使用同一语言的完全相同的两份副本（共享相同文本和词元分割），但映射到不相交（disjoint）的词元空间。据此验证词元空间不重叠是否为知识分隔的决定性因素。之后提出将语言映射到共享词元空间（通过简单逐词翻译）的干预方法。

Result: 发现跨语言知识泛化不佳的问题在预训练早期即出现，且标准干预无法解决。关键发现：即使同一语言的相同副本，只要词元空间不重叠，也会引发知识分隔，证明不重叠的词元空间是跨语言知识泛化的根本障碍。将语言映射到共享词元空间后，跨语言知识泛化显著改善，可恢复高达12.6%的母语学习效率，是基线水平的14倍。

Conclusion: 词元空间的不重叠是导致LLM跨语言知识泛化受限的根本原因。通过将不同语言映射到共享词元空间（如逐词翻译），可以有效提升跨语言知识迁移能力，为改进多语模型训练提供了明确的指导方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress in the processing and modeling of many languages. Yet, unlike human multilinguals, they exhibit surprisingly limited cross-lingual knowledge transfer. While this limitation is well documented, its origins during multilingual training remain unclear. We pretrain 360M- and 7B-parameter LLMs and show that poor cross-lingual knowledge generalization emerges during pretraining and persists under standard interventions. To isolate its cause, we employ a controlled bilingual pretraining setting using two copies of the same language, sharing identical text and token segmentation, but mapped to disjoint token spaces. We find that disjoint tokens alone are enough to induce knowledge compartmentalization, even between identical copies of the same language, establishing disjoint token spaces as a fundamental barrier to cross-lingual knowledge generalization. Guided by this understanding, we suggest mapping languages into a shared token space by simple word-wise translation and find it substantially improves cross-lingual knowledge generalization, recovering up to 12.6\% of native-language learning efficiency --- 14$\times$ the baseline.

</details>


### [116] [A frontend-backend architecture for tool calls in full-duplex speech models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19334)
*Ke Hu,Slyne Deng,Chen Chen,Elena Rastorgueva,Edresson Casanova,Punit Kumar,Dharmendra Choudhary,Nikhil Srihari,Ameya Sunil Mahabaleshwarkar,Viet Anh Trinh,Slim Essid,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Zhehuai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种前端-后端架构，在双工语音对话中实现外部工具调用：双工语音转文本（S2T）前端模型学习发出“委托令牌”，将流式ASR转录转发给基于文本的后端LLM进行工具调用，再通过轻量级“prefill-and-repeat”机制将工具结果注入前端，最终以流式TTS合成给用户，在保持自然双工交互的同时实现强工具调用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音到语音（S2S）模型能提供自然、低延迟的对话交互，但缺乏使用外部工具和完成语音智能体任务的能力。现有方法难以在保持双工交互特性的同时集成工具调用，因此需要一种既能维持自然双工对话又能实现强大智能体工具调用能力的模块化方案。

Method: 采用前端-后端分离架构：（1）双工S2T前端模型学习发出“委托令牌”（delegation token），并将流式ASR转录转发给基于文本的后端LLM用于工具调用；（2）后端返回的工具调用结果通过轻量级“prefill-and-repeat”机制重新注入前端；（3）前端使用流式TTS将结果合成为语音反馈给用户。该方案对前端模型修改极少，从而保留了常规双工轮流发言、中断处理和低延迟交互能力。

Result: 在单轮工具调用评估中，系统实现92-97%的工具调用召回率、有竞争力的工具调用预测性能，以及对无关调用的81.2%拒绝准确率。配备更大后端（如Qwen3-235B-A22B）时，在Full-Duplex-Bench-V3上与开源和闭源模型相比取得有竞争力的结果，并在EVA-Bench上显著优于GPT-realtime-mini和Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct。

Conclusion: 后端委托是结合自然双工语音交互与强大智能体工具调用能力的有效且模块化的方法，证明在保持双工对话性能的同时，通过后端LLM实现工具调用是可行的架构路径。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech-to-speech (S2S) models provide natural, low-latency conversational interaction and would benefit from the ability to use external tools and complete voice-agent tasks. We propose a frontend-backend architecture where a duplex speech-to-text frontend learns to emit a delegation token and forwards streaming ASR transcripts to a text-based backend LLM for tool calls. Tool-call results from the backend are injected back into the frontend through a lightweight prefill-and-repeat mechanism and then synthesized using streaming TTS to the user. Our approach largely preserves regular duplex turn-taking, interruption handling, and low-latency interaction as it requires minimal modifications to the frontend model. In a single-turn tool-call evaluation, our system achieves 92-97% tool-call recall, competitive tool-call prediction performance, and 81.2% accuracy in rejecting irrelevant calls. When equipped with a larger backend (e.g., Qwen3-235B-A22B), our system achieves competitive results on Full-Duplex-Bench-V3 compared to open and closed source models, and significantly outperforms GPT-realtime-mini and Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct on EVA-Bench. These results demonstrate that backend delegation is an effective and modular approach for combining natural duplex speech interaction with strong agentic tool-call capabilities.

</details>


### [117] [The Role of Fine-grained Harm Signals in LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19366)
*Soyeon Park,Seogyeong Jeong,Sunwoo Kim,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）中按风险类别划分的有害性表示，重点探讨了去除共享一般有害性表示后得到的"类别残差"的作用。通过在3个指令微调LLM的11个风险类别上使用激活引导，发现类别残差编码有害性和引发拒绝的能力因类别而异，这些模式在模型间具有相似性或模型依赖性。此外，类别残差还能增强LLM下游与共享一般有害性表示的内部对齐。研究表明，除一般有害性表示外，更细粒度的类别残差也应纳入LLM安全研究的考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明LLM内部的有害性表示在不同风险类别间存在差异，同时共享一个通用的一般有害性表示成分。这引发了一个关键问题：类别特定成分在一般有害性表示之外究竟扮演什么角色？现有工作未能回答这一层次化分解中各成分的功能差异。

Method: 作者从每个类别有害性表示中减去共享的一般有害性表示，从而在每个层上得到与一般有害性表示正交的"类别残差"。随后在3个指令微调LLM的11个风险类别上使用激活引导（activation steering）技术，系统性地检验这些类别残差编码有害性和诱发拒绝的能力。

Result: 研究发现：1）类别残差是否编码有害性因类别而异，且这种类别级别模式在模型间具有相似性；2）类别残差是否引发拒绝也因类别而异，但该模式更具模型依赖性；3）类别残差能够增强LLM下游与共享一般有害性表示的内部对齐。这表明与主干概念正交的方向也能对概念的下游放大产生贡献。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论：为了全面理解LLM安全，除了共享的一般有害性表示外，还应重视更细粒度的类别残差。更广泛地，该发现揭示了一个重要原理——即使在某一层与概念正交的方向，也能在后续层中对概念表达起到放大作用，这对理解表示分解与安全对齐机制具有重要意义。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that internal harmfulness representations in large language models vary across risk categories, while sharing a common general harm representation component. This raises a question about the role of the category-specific component beyond general harm representation in LLM safety. To answer this question, we isolate the category-specific component by removing shared general harmfulness representation from each categorical harmfulness representation, yielding a category residual that is orthogonal to general harmfulness at every layer. Using activation steering with category residuals across 11 risk categories in 3 instruction-tuned LLMs, we find that whether category residuals encode harmfulness varies across categories, and that this category-wise pattern is similar across models. Whether category residuals induce refusal also varies across categories, but this category-wise pattern is more model-dependent. We also find that category residuals increase LLMs' downstream internal alignment with shared general harmfulness representation. Together, these findings demonstrate that more fine-grained category residuals should also be considered beyond shared general harmfulness representation to fully understand LLM safety. More broadly, our findings show that even a direction orthogonal to a concept at one layer can contribute to the concept's downstream amplification.

</details>


### [118] [Less Is More: Graph-free Multimodal RAG via Multi-signal Late Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19417)
*Tithi Rakshit,Hongkuan Zhou,Lavdim Halilaj,Yuqicheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TrioRAG是一种无图的多模态检索增强生成框架，通过融合三种互补信号（问题、锚点图像、由两者生成的VLM增强查询）在共享多向量索引上独立检索并晚期融合，在成本更低、推理速度提升1.6-2.3倍的情况下媲美或超越基于图的系统；同时提出了AutoQA多模态汽车基准测试，其问题扎根于语料库外的网络图像，实验表明图像检索文档级召回率仅19.3%，而文本信号尤其VLM增强查询能保持检索鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的RAG虽广泛应用于多模态、跨文档问答，但构建语料库级图谱成本高昂、查询缓慢且难以维护，因此需要探索更高效、可维护的无图替代方案。

Method: 提出TrioRAG无图框架：利用三个互补检索信号（问题、锚点图像、由两者生成的VLM增强查询），各信号在共享的页面文本与页面图像多向量索引上独立检索，再通过晚期融合整合结果；并引入AutoQA多模态汽车基准，其问题基于有噪声的网络图像，需要跨手册推理。

Result: 在三个基准上，TrioRAG匹配或超越基于图的系统，同时降低总成本并将每查询推理加速1.6-2.3倍；在AutoQA中，图像检索文档级召回率仅19.3%，而文本信号尤其VLM增强查询维持了检索鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 无图的多信号融合检索范式在成本与速度上优于图方法且性能不逊色；AutoQA作为模型策展而非人工验证的测试平台，揭示了纯图像检索的局限，凸显文本信号（尤其VLM增强查询）对鲁棒检索的关键作用。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely used for multimodal, cross-document question answering. However, building corpus-level graphs is expensive, slow to query, and difficult to maintain. We present TrioRAG, a graph-free multimodal framework that integrates evidence from three complementary signals: the question, the anchor image, and a VLM-enhanced query generated from both. Each signal retrieves independently over a shared multi-vector index of page text and page images, and the results are combined through late fusion. Further, we introduce AutoQA, a multimodal automotive benchmark whose questions are grounded in noisy, web-sourced images rather than clean document-sourced figures. Its questions require reasoning across manuals. We position it as a model-curated testbed rather than a human-validated gold standard. Across three benchmarks, TrioRAG matches or outperforms graph-based systems while reducing total cost and accelerating per-query inference by 1.6-2.3 times. By construction, AutoQA grounds its questions in out-of-corpus web images. In this setting image retrieval reaches only 19.3% document-level recall, while text-derived signals, especially the VLM-enhanced query, keep retrieval robust.

</details>


### [119] [For Your Eyes Only: Evaluating Coordination Between Isolated Language Model Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19504)
*Alexander Shirnin,Aleksey Kudelya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了"仅限你看到的眼睛"（For Your Eyes Only）合作信号博弈，用于评估一个模型能否在自然语言中嵌入信号，使另一个独立实例的同类模型仅依靠共享预训练和任务指令就能检测到该信号，无需共享记忆或专门的协调训练。在300个词对和7个当代模型上的实验显示，多数模型在需要避免可检测信号时难以维持协调，而一个前沿模型在过滤后仍保持近乎完美的性能；模型还能将这种能力用于故意误导，且跨架构的协调一致性弱于架构内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型生成的内容在自动化工作流中被其他模型实例越来越频繁地消费，产生了一个实际重要的问题：一个模型能否在自然语言中嵌入一种信号，使同一模型的独立实例仅依靠共享的预训练和任务指令就能检测到该信号，而无需任何共享记忆或协调特定的训练。这一问题的研究对理解和利用模型间的隐式协调能力具有重要价值。

Method: 作者设计了一个合作信号博弈：发送者（Sender）为两个词产生自由形式的描述，其中一个是隐藏目标；隔离的接收者（Receiver）必须识别该目标。在来自既定心理语言学语料库的300个词对上评估了四个架构家族的七个当代模型，采用双通道成功率（Double-Pass Success Rate）来控制系统输出偏差。

Result: 多数模型在必须避免可检测信号时难以维持协调，而一个前沿模型在过滤后仍保持近乎完美的性能。此外，模型能将这种能力导向故意误导，且跨架构的协调一致性始终弱于架构内部的协调一致性。

Conclusion: 研究证明模型间确实存在这种隐式的、仅依赖共享预训练和指令的协调能力，但该能力在多数模型中较弱、在架构间不稳定，仅个别前沿模型能稳健保留；同时该能力可被用于误导，提示需要关注相关的安全隐患。

Abstract: As model-generated content is increasingly consumed by other model instances in automated workflows, a practically important question arises: can a model embed a signal in natural language that an independent instance of the same model can detect, relying only on shared pre-training and task instructions, without any shared memory or coordination-specific training? We introduce For Your Eyes Only, a cooperative signalling game designed to evaluate this directly. A Sender produces free-form descriptions for two words, one of which is a hidden target; an isolated Receiver must identify it. We evaluate seven contemporary models from four architectural families on 300 word pairs from established psycholinguistic corpora, using the Double-Pass Success Rate to control for output biases. We find that most models struggle to maintain coordination once they are required to avoid detectable signals, while one frontier model retains near-perfect performance even after such filtering. We further show that models can direct this capability toward deliberate misdirection, and that coordination is consistently weaker across architectures than within them.

</details>


### [120] [From Parameters to Behaviors: A Survey of Model Fusion for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19553)
*Shuo Cai,Yanggan Gu,Zihao Wang,Yuanyi Wang,Yibo Yan,Wenjun Wang,Yuhang Liu,Guanghao Zhu,Sirui Huang,Ming Li,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于模型融合（Model Fusion）的综述论文。截至2026年6月，Hugging Face平台上已拥有超过200万个模型，为模型复用与能力集成提供了丰富基础。然而现有综述仅覆盖该领域的部分分支，缺乏统一定义与系统分类。该综述首次定义了"模型融合"概念，并将其划分为三个层次：参数级融合、表示级融合和行为级融合，同时回顾了相关指标、基准与应用，总结了当前挑战并指出了未来研究方向。论文还提供了涵盖模型融合相关论文的完整列表（GitHub链接）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Hugging Face平台模型数量突破200万，模型复用和能力集成日益重要，但该领域的综述文献零散、只覆盖局部，缺乏统一的定义和系统性分类框架。作者旨在填补这一空白，为该领域提供清晰的地图，并支撑后续研究。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法：首先提出模型融合（Model Fusion）的统一定义，然后依据融合发生的层级构建了三级系统分类体系——参数级融合（parameter-level）、表示级融合（representation-level）和行为级融合（behavior-level）——在此基础上梳理相关评估指标、基准测试和应用场景，并归纳当前挑战与未来方向。

Result: 论文提出了模型融合的统一定义和三级分类体系（参数级、表示级、行为级），系统性地整理了该领域的前人工作，并汇总了相关指标、基准与应用，同时识别出当前面临的主要挑战和未来待探索的研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述为模型融合领域提供了清晰、统一的研究图谱，有助于整合分散的研究成果，指导后续研究者在统一定义与分类框架下开展更深层的工作。作者公开了相关论文清单资源以支持社区持续更新。

Abstract: Model fusion integrates the capabilities from source models into a single target model. As of June 2026, Hugging Face hosts more than 2M models. This growing pool provides a rich base for model reuse and capability integration. Yet existing surveys often cover only separate parts of this space, and they do not provide a unified definition or a systematic taxonomy. This survey defines model fusion and organizes prior work into three levels: parameter-level, representation-level, and behavior-level fusion. We also review related metrics, benchmarks, and applications, summarize current challenges, and identify future directions. Our goal is to provide a clear map of this area and support future work on model fusion. A comprehensive list of papers about model fusion is available at https://github.com/Baicaihaochi/Awesome-Model-Fusion-Survey.

</details>


### [121] [CliniCIRCA: A Modular LLM Framework for Constructing Longitudinal Mental Health Patient Journeys from Raw EHR Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19585)
*Aiwei Ivy Zhang,Nimra Ishfaq,Mohit Chandra,Santiago Alvarez Lesmes,Adam Coscia,Khatiya Chelidze Moon,Xiaohan Ding,Munmun De Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CliniCIRCA，一个用于日历锚定、不精确感知的临床年鉴重建的多阶段LLM框架，首次在没有事件级时间戳的情况下对非结构化出院小结中的临床事件进行时间分类。从14,882份MIMIC-III精神健康住院记录中构建基准，在52份出院小结上生成15,891个带时间标签的事件，通过临床医生在环评估纠正629处错误得到金标准标签，并将时间线压缩1.52倍为按日期分组的编年记录，最终扩展到1,000条银标准时间线作为训练数据，证明指令调优优于零样本和少样本提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在精神健康护理中，临床医生需要对患者旅程（涵盖生物、心理、社会事件的纵向进展）进行推理，但这些事件常常分散在不同非结构化文本叙述中，使时间恢复变得困难。现有方法依赖事件级时间戳，而现实中临床事件通常没有明确标注时间。

Method: 提出多阶段LLM框架CliniCIRCA，包含时间分类、临床医生在环标注纠错和基于时间线的摘要生成等阶段。从14,882份MIMIC-III精神健康住院记录中构建52份出院小结基准，并通过对比零样本/少样本提示与指令调优（涉及五种开放权重模型）评估事件抽取、时间标注和摘要任务。

Result: 在52份出院小结上生成15,891个时间标注事件；经临床医生在环评估纠正629个错误并产生验证的金标准标签；纠正后的时间线将源文本压缩1.52倍为按日期分组的编年记录；扩展到1,000条银标准时间线作为训练数据。相比零样本和少样本提示，指令调优在五种开放权重模型上普遍改善了事件抽取、时间标注和摘要性能（经银标准与临床医生验证评估）。

Conclusion: CliniCIRCA证明无需事件级时间戳即可实现临床事件的日历锚定时间分类，其时间线驱动摘要和时间线大规模生成（银标准）均可行，且指令调优是提升开权重模型时序理解能力的有效途径。

Abstract: In mental health care, reasoning over patient journeys is a key task for clinicians. Yet these journeys, encompassing a longitudinal progression of biological, psychological, and social events, are often spread across disparate unstructured text narratives, making temporal recovery challenging. We present CliniCIRCA, a multi-stage LLM framework for Calendar-anchored, Imprecision-aware Reconstruction of Clinical Annals. To our knowledge, CliniCIRCA is the first to temporally classify clinical events across unstructured discharge summaries without event-level timestamps. From 14,882 MIMIC-III mental health admissions, we first construct a benchmark of 52 discharge summaries on which CliniCIRCA produces 15,891 temporally tagged events. After correcting 629 errors based on a clinician-in-the-loop evaluation, we produce verified gold-standard labels. Finally, the corrected timelines drive a temporally grounded summarization stage that compresses each source 1.52 times into a date-grouped chronological record. We then scale the framework to generate 1,000 silver-standard timelines and evaluate them as training data. Compared with zero- and few-shot prompting, instruction tuning generally improves five open-weight models on event extraction, temporal tagging, and summarization across silver and clinician-verified evaluations.

</details>


### [122] [Form Over Content In Gradient-Based Data Attribution Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19589)
*Sunwoo Kim,Seokwon Jung,Sohyung Kim,Seong Joon Oh,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过独立变化任务与答案格式，证明基于梯度相似性的数据归因方法主要追踪的是"答案格式"（surface form）而非任务语义。同一基准被渲染成不同答案格式类别时，梯度对齐度接近0；共享同一答案格式的基准对则对齐显著。此现象在预训练检查点、后训练、模型规模与家族中均成立，且LESS数据选择方法的选择结果过度代表了每类目标自身的答案格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于梯度相似性数据归因方法（广泛用于大语言模型训练数据的分析与选择）究竟衡量什么存在争议：一派认为是任务相关技能，另一派报告表面形式是主要因素。作者旨在通过独立控制任务与答案格式两个变量来解决这一争议。

Method: 将基准测试渲染为不同的答案格式类别，构造出"共享任务但不共享格式"或"共享格式但不共享任务"的基准对，从而独立地测量梯度对齐与答案格式、任务语义之间的关系。考察从最早预训练检查点到后训练、跨模型规模与家族的一致性，并分析LESS（基于梯度的指令微调数据选择方法）的实际选择结果。

Result: 发现梯度对齐严格跟随答案格式：共享答案格式的基准对具有强对齐（去衰减余弦约0.4），而同一基准以不同答案格式类别渲染时对齐接近0。该顺序在预训练早期检查点至后训练全程、跨模型规模和家族均成立。LESS的选择结果过度代表了每个目标自身的答案格式。

Conclusion: 基于梯度的归因方法追踪的是格式相似性而非任务语义，因此这类方法及其对梯度的语义解释应建立在"答案格式与任务独立变化"的数据上进行测试，以获得更强的鲁棒性与可靠性。

Abstract: Data attribution methods using gradient similarity are widely used to analyze and select training data for large language models, but what gradient similarity actually measures is debated. Some interpret it as identifying task-relevant skills, while other work reports that surface form is the main factor. We resolve this debate for supervised fine-tuning examples by varying task and answer format independently. Specifically, we render benchmarks in different answer formats, such that datasets can share a task without a format or a format without a task. We find that gradient alignment follows the answer format, as benchmark pairs sharing an answer format align strongly (disattenuated cosine near 0.4), while same benchmarks rendered with different answer format classes show no alignment (near 0.0). We demonstrate that this ordering holds from the earliest pretraining checkpoints through post-training, and across model scales and families. We then analyze the released selections of LESS, a gradient-based data selection method for instruction tuning, and find that each target's selections over-represent the target's own answer format. Hence, we demonstrate that gradient-based attribution methods track format similarity more than task semantics, meaning that such methods, as well as the semantic interpretation of the gradient, should be tested on data where answer format and task vary independently for greater robustness and reliability.

</details>


### [123] [Full-Duplex Speech Models Take the Floor When Asked, Not When Needed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19596)
*Linkai Peng,Baorian Nuchged,Kaiqi Fu,Yuyang Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究全双工语音模型在对话中主动发言的能力，发现它们对被点名和静默的响应可靠，但对虚假事实或危险等实义触发词响应不足，且在内容回应上缺乏干预，存在话语启动和内容理解的双重差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型需同时听与说，但如何决定何时发言（如纠错、补词、警示）尚未明确。作者希望检验模型是否具备类似人类的自选发言能力，并区分“发言理由”与“发言机会”。

Method: 构建主题匹配的英语独白，仅改变触发句，定义10种基于话轮分配规则的场景，压缩词间停顿以限制静默机会。对5个模型家族进行测试，并分析Moshi和PersonaPlex的帧级文本词元概率及回应内容。

Result: 被点名和静默远比虚假事实或危险更能可靠触发发言；在触发结束后的前2秒内，Moshi和PersonaPlex对虚假事实的文本词元概率低于中性条件。即使给予暂停或允许打断也未能弥补差距。在有话轮时，模型能回答大多数直接问题，但非空回应中挑战虚假事实的比例仅0.14–0.15，对危险发出警告的比例仅0.04–0.07。

Conclusion: 全双工模型在发言触发和回应内容两方面均存在明显不足，需真正的内容理解与基于理解的干预决策来弥合差距。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models listen and speak at once, promising always-on assistants. Yet they must also decide when they should speak. Human listeners speak when addressed or when the speaker stops, but also self-select to correct a false claim, supply a missing word, or warn of danger. We ask whether full-duplex models do the same. To separate the reason to speak from the opportunity, we construct context-matched English monologues in which only the trigger utterance varies within a topic, define 10 conditions from turn-allocation rules, and compress inter-word pauses to limit opportunities created by silence. Across five model families, being addressed and silence are far more reliable triggers than false facts or hazards. Frame-level text-token probabilities in Moshi and PersonaPlex are lower for false facts than for Neutral when averaged over the first 2\,s after trigger end. Pauses or permission to interrupt do not close this gap either. Given the floor, Moshi and PersonaPlex answer most direct questions, yet the proportion of non-empty false-fact replies that challenge the claim is only .14--.15, and the proportion of hazard replies that warn of danger is .04--.07. This paper thus identifies a gap in both speech initiation and response content. Closing it requires genuine content understanding and intervention decisions grounded in it.

</details>


### [124] [Chain-of-Thought Entropy as a Reliability Signal: A Preregistered Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19606)
*Theodore O. Cochran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是对Zhao（2026）报道的"思维链熵轨迹形状预测正确性，而总熵降幅不预测"这一解离现象的独立复现研究。研究在完整GSM8K与MATH-500测试集上，用四个开放权重模型（含一个推理蒸馏模型）进行了OSF预注册的复现实验。结果发现形状信号可复现，但幅度信号随设定而分化：在锚定模型上两者表现不一致（GSM8K上熵降幅与正确性秩相关为-0.018，MATH-500上为+0.414），而推理蒸馏模型上二元形状信号几乎不触发。研究还发现最终步熵在八个模型×基准单元格上均优于二元形状标志（按ROC面积），并记录了7项协议差异和4项原始论文未报告的协议依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复现Zhao在2026年报道的"熵轨迹形状可预测正确性、熵总降幅不能预测"这一解离现象，因为该解离的"幅度"半边仅建立在单一模型、单一种子、300题的一次运行上，证据薄弱；而"形状"半边则已在全规模两个基准及第二个模型家族上报道过，值得独立验证。尤其原始研究未测试推理蒸馏类模型，本研究加以补足。

Method: 在OSF上预先注册后开展确认性运行，跨越完整的GSM8K和MATH-500测试集，使用四个开放权重模型（含一个推理蒸馏模型）。比较二元形状信号（单调与非单调思维链的准确率差）与总熵降幅信号（与正确性的秩相关），并补充用分级违规计数及最终步熵作为预测因子的探究性比较，以ROC面积和风险-覆盖率面积为指标，评估八种模型×基准组合。

Result: 形状信号成功复现。幅度信号随设定分化：锚定模型上单调与非单调链的准确率差为+9.6个百分点（GSM8K）和+27.5个百分点（MATH-500）；总熵降幅与正确性的秩相关在GSM8K为-0.018、MATH-500为+0.414，表明幅度信号不稳定。推理蒸馏模型上二元形状信号仅约每百条链触发一次，无法估计注册对比，但分级违规计数仍具预测力。探究性比较中，最终步熵在全部八个模型×基准单元格上按ROC面积优于二元形状标志，在风险-覆盖率面积上多数（六或七个）单元格占优。

Conclusion: 本研究在全测试集规模、7项文档化协议差异下实现了形状信号的复现，绘制了幅度信号成立与失效的设定图谱，并提供了4项原始论文未报告的协议依赖性测量。总体而言，熵轨迹"形状"信号的可复现性强于"总降幅"信号，且最终步熵可能是一种更稳健的替代预测指标。

Abstract: This empirical study is an independent reproduction of the dissociation Zhao reported in 2026. The shape of a large language model's chain-of-thought entropy trajectory predicts whether the final answer is correct, while the magnitude of its total entropy drop does not. The dissociation merits reproduction because the magnitude half rests on a single 300-problem run with one model at one seed, while the shape half was reported at full scale on both benchmarks and on a second model family. Registered at OSF before any confirmatory run, the reproduction crosses the complete GSM8K and MATH-500 benchmark test sets with four open-weight models including one reasoning-distilled model of a kind the original did not test. The shape signal replicates. The magnitude signal divides by setting. On the anchor model the accuracy gap between monotone and non-monotone chains is +9.6 percentage points on GSM8K and +27.5 on MATH-500, while the rank correlation of the total entropy drop with correctness is -0.018 on GSM8K and +0.414 on MATH-500. On the reasoning-distilled model the binary form of the shape signal fires on about one chain in a hundred, too few to estimate the registered contrast, while the graded violation count remains predictive there. In an exploratory comparison the final-step entropy alone outperforms the binary shape flag in all eight model-by-benchmark cells by ROC area, and in six or seven by the risk-coverage area the original reports, depending on an integration range the original does not state. The study contributes a reproduction of the shape signal at full test-set scale under seven documented protocol differences, a map of the settings where the magnitude signal holds and fails, and measurements of four protocol dependencies the original does not report.

</details>


### [125] [Semantic Layer Induction from Raw Telemetry via Hierarchical LLM and RAG Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19615)
*Yuanzhe Jia,Ali Anaissi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端框架，无需标注数据和人工规则，即可从应用原始日志中全自动构建业务语义层，通过两阶段语义抽象（LLM推断高层业务特征 + 结构化管线推导细粒度业务节点），在生产级遥测数据上将人工评估的语义质量从50分提升至80+分、维护成本降低80%、过滤74%噪声，并达到0.87的Cohen's kappa一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用产生海量原始遥测数据，但将嘈杂、异构的事件流转化为可执行的业务洞察仍是一项根本性挑战。数据工程师和分析师需要耗费大量精力去调和语义差异、手工编写解析逻辑、维护原始数据与业务KPI之间脆弱的映射关系。现有工作缺乏从原始遥测数据自动归纳业务语义层的方法。

Method: 提出一个端到端框架，采用两阶段语义抽象：(1) 通过结合领域行业知识的LLM推断识别高层业务特征；(2) 通过结构化管线（数据精炼、混合检索、多阶段过滤、语义聚类、规范命名）推导细粒度业务节点。系统无需标注训练数据或人工规则工程。

Result: 在生产级遥测数据上的评估表明：系统将人工评估的语义质量从50分提升到80+分（100分制），减少80%的维护工作，过滤74%的噪声，并通过集成的LLM-as-Judge评估达到0.87的Cohen's kappa，实现持续、可扩展的质量保证。

Conclusion: 该工作区别于以往研究，创新性地解决了从原始遥测数据归纳业务语义层这一新问题，且无需标注训练数据或人工规则工程，为业务语义层的自动构建提供了可行且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Modern applications generate massive volumes of raw telemetry data, but translating those noisy, heterogeneous event streams into actionable business insights remains a fundamental challenge. Data engineers and analysts expend substantial effort reconciling semantic discrepancies, hand-crafting parsing logics, and maintaining fragile mappings between raw data and business KPIs. In this paper, we present an end-to-end framework that fully automates the construction of a business semantic layer from application raw logs. Our approach introduces a two-stage semantic abstraction: first, high-level business features are identified via LLM inference augmented with domain-specific industry knowledge; second, fine-grained business nodes are derived through a structured pipeline comprising data refinement, hybrid retrieval, multi-stage filtering, semantic clustering, and canonical naming. Evaluation on production-scale telemetry demonstrates that our system improves human-assessed semantic quality from 50 to 80+ on a 100-point scale, reduces maintenance effort by 80%, filters out 74% of noise, and achieves 0.87 Cohen's kappa via an integrated LLM-as-Judge evaluation, enabling continuous, scalable quality assurance. Overall, our work distinguishes itself from prior work by addressing the novel problem of business semantic layer induction from raw telemetry, operating without labeled training data or manual rule engineering.

</details>


### [126] [Improving Cross-Lingual Transfer for Sequential Sentence Classification in Research Papers via Structural Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19650)
*Kazuhiro Yamauchi,Marie Katsurai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文研究了跨语言迁移在序列句子分类（SSC）任务中的应用。作者构建了涵盖13种非英语语言的多语言SSC数据集，系统探究影响迁移成功的因素，发现语言邻近度对迁移性能无一致预测能力，而修辞组织的结构相似性呈现弱但一致的正相关；在控制源语言性能后，标签分布相似性是最一致的预测因子。基于此发现，作者提出三种显式利用结构信息的生成式方法，在域内评估中与最强编码器基线持平，在迁移到未见语言时超越最强编码器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列句子分类是结构化科学文献的重要任务，但非英语语言训练数据稀缺。跨语言迁移是解决该问题的可行方案，然而以往研究多关注语言相似性的作用，未充分考虑SSC固有的语篇层模式（如标签序列与位置规律）。作者旨在系统考察决定SSC跨语言迁移成功的因素，从而改善多语言数字图书馆中科学知识的可及性。

Method: (1) 从五个学术数据库构建覆盖13种非英语语言的多语言SSC数据集。(2) 使用编码器模型和生成式模型开展跨语言迁移实验，考察语言邻近度、修辞组织结构相似性、标签分布相似性等因素对迁移性能的预测能力。(3) 基于结构信息在迁移中起关键作用的发现，提出三种显式利用结构信息的生成式方法。

Result: 实验表明：语言邻近度对迁移性能无一致的预测能力；修辞组织结构的相似性对迁移性能呈弱但一致的正相关；在控制源语言性能后，标签分布相似性是最一致的预测因子。所提方法组合在域内评估中与最强编码器基线持平，在向训练时未见语言的迁移中超越最强编码器基线。

Conclusion: 与语言层面的相似性相比，语篇/结构层面的信息（标签分布与修辞组织）才是决定SSC跨语言迁移成功的更可靠因素。生成式模型通过显式利用结构信息，可取得优于或持平于编码器方法的迁移性能，为多语言SSC的稀疏资源场景提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Sequential sentence classification (SSC) is an essential task for structuring scientific publications, and extending SSC research to languages other than English can improve accessibility to scientific knowledge in multilingual digital libraries. Cross-lingual transfer is a promising approach to address the scarcity of training data in non-English languages. Prior work on other natural language processing tasks has shown the benefits of capturing linguistic similarity between source and target languages. However, SSC inherently depends on patterns at the discourse level, such as label sequences and positional regularities, which appear consistently across languages regardless of linguistic differences. To examine the factors that determine transfer success in SSC, we constructed a multilingual SSC dataset covering 13 non-English languages collected from five academic databases. Our cross-lingual transfer experiments, using both encoder-based and generative models, show that linguistic proximity has no consistent predictive power for transfer performance, whereas structural similarity in rhetorical organization shows a weak but consistent positive correlation across models. After controlling for source-language performance, the similarity of label distributions is the most consistent predictor. Building on this finding, we propose a set of three methods that explicitly leverage structural information using generative models. In the in-domain evaluation, the best combination reaches parity with the strongest encoder baselines, and in transfer to languages unseen during training, it outperforms the strongest encoder baseline.

</details>


### [127] [A Phonemically Comprehensive, ASCII-Only Romanization Scheme for Thai and Lao: Systematic Cross-Lingual Correspondence and Chinese-User-Friendly Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19736)
*Zijie Zhang,Tan Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一套仅使用ASCII字符、音位全面的泰语和老挝语罗马化方案，将这两种关系密切的语言作为统一的跨语言设计问题。该方案在保持"一符号一音位"透明性的同时，系统地对应泰语和老挝语，并兼顾与拼音、粤拼的音系对应关系。声调采用紧凑的个位数字默认记号，并辅以可选的调值与历史调类表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泰语和老挝语的罗马化方案在表示音位（段音对立、元音长度、声调）方面存在不足，且未将两种语言作为统一的跨语言问题进行设计，难以满足语言学习和跨语言语音处理对可读、可键入、可机处理音位表征的需求。

Method: 提出一套ASCII-only的罗马化体系，统一处理泰语和老挝语：它覆盖段音对立、元音长度和词汇声调，遵循"一符号一音位"的透明原则；优先采用共时语音对应（含与拼音、粤拼的对应），在不违背语音透明性的前提下保留历时语音对应；声调采用紧凑的个位数默认表示，另提供可选的调值表示和历史调类表示。

Result: 该方案在泰语和老挝语之间建立了系统的对应关系，实现了音位表征的透明性、与拼音和粤拼的兼容性，以及声调的可选多层级表示，最终得到一种可读、键盘友好、可机器处理的音位表征形式。

Conclusion: 该罗马化方案为泰语和老挝语提供了兼具语音透明性、跨语言系统对应和实用性（可读、可键入、可机处理）的音位表征，适用于语言学习和跨语言语音处理领域。

Abstract: This paper proposes a phonemically comprehensive, ASCII-only romanization scheme for Thai and Lao, treating the two closely related languages as a unified cross-lingual design problem. The scheme represents segmental contrasts, vowel length, and lexical tone while maintaining one-symbol-one-phoneme transparency and systematic correspondence between Thai and Lao. The scheme prioritizes synchronic phonetic correspondence, including correspondence with Pinyin and Jyutping where applicable, while preserving historical-phonological correspondence where it does not conflict with phonetic transparency. Tone uses a compact single-digit default notation, supplemented by optional tone-value and historical tone-category representations. The resulting scheme provides a readable, keyboard-friendly, and machine-processable phonemic representation for language learning and cross-lingual speech processing.

</details>


### [128] [Learn Before You Judge: Progressive Knowledge-to-Decision Alignment for Explainable Hateful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19778)
*Bo Xu,Chenyuan Wang,Xinyu Chen,Quanhao Zhu,Rui Lin,Liang Zhao,Hongfei Lin,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProKDA是一种面向可解释仇恨表情包检测的渐进式知识到决策对齐方法。它通过智能体背景知识构建管道获取外部知识，并采用三阶段训练策略（背景知识学习、仇恨检测学习、仇恨边界对齐），逐步将背景知识转化为稳健的检测决策，在三个公开基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释仇恨表情包检测的"先解释后检测"方法常将解释生成与标签预测耦合在同一训练过程中，导致任务目标之间产生干扰，检测性能受限甚至不及简单的SFT基线。
